2a1(rgyud): //dkyil 'khor dang ni thams cad kyi//sbyi yi cho ga rgyas pa dag///
2a2(rgyud): gas pa'i dga' ba 'khor gang //rab tu gnas pa las gnas pa dag sogs 
2a3(rgyud): kyis bshad pa gang //'jig rten dag ba rtse phyir ngas//rnam par gnod pa la sogs pa//dkyil
2b1(rgyud): stong pa ni//lnga brgya bshad pa gang yin pa//dkyil 'khor de dag rnams spyi yi//tshig 'di rnams ngas
2b2(rgyud): bshad//'khor thams cad bri bar bya//slob dpon bstan nas kyang shes pa//rnal
2b3(rgyud): khrims ldan nas bcom ldan 'das rnams myangs can//dad dang dbu la ni mi mos pas 
2b4(rgyud): rigs so so la yang //mdzes dang rin zhing de bzhin gshegs pa la 
2b5(rgyud): bzang //
2b6(rgyud): rigs che la mos pa can//gsang sngags spyod pa la bslabs shing //
3a1(rgyud): @#/ O//Ika bya brag shes//slob ma dag gi tshul shes shing//de bzhin gsang sngags rgyud dang 'dzin//rjes su gan('a)
3a2(rgyud): zhing dbang bskuIla//chog shes bas brjod dbang lhur ldan pa //slob dpon rigs la bsten pa kur byas//dkyil 'khor thams
3a3(rgyud): cad khong du chud//gsang sngags kun dang bla ma dang //de bzhin blo bur 'gon mchod byed//phyag rgya la sogs bcing
3a4(rgyud): ba dang //dkyil 'khor bri ba'i las rnams dang//gsang sngags mchod pa'i spyod pa la//slob dpon gy-is ni mkhas 
3a5(rgyud): par bya//de nas gzhan yang bshad bya ni//sa yi phyogs kyi mtshan nyid te//sa bzhi ci 'dra bzang ba dang//mi bzang ba
3b1(rgyud): dag bsgrags pa'o//mthon dman can dang tsher ma can//gyo mo can dang dag dang //ngam 'grog rus pa sdom dum khung//gro gar thal ba tsha rko can
3b2(rgyud): gseg ma rdo dang rdul dang bsra//srin bu grog sbur la swogs mang //de lta bu'i sa bzhi ni//mkhas pas rnam pa kun la spangs//mthon dman snyoMs
3b3(rgyud): shing rtsang la snum//zug rngu'i nyes pa rnam par spangs//shar du byang du gzhol ba yi//sa gzhi las rnams kun la shes//dang
3b4(rgyud): po pus nub tsam rgos la//de nyid kyis ni dgang bar bya//gal te lhag par gyur na ni//dngos grub dag ni yong bar spyad
3b5(rgyud): de las bzlog par gyur na ni//bsgrub pa pos ni las mi rtsams//rtsams nas sdug bsngal myos 'gyur zhing //legs pa dag
3b6(rgyud): kyang mi thob po//dkyil 'khor sa bzhi gang dag ni//nye 'khor dag na chu me pa//de ni shis shing dngos grub thob//chu
4a1(rgyud): @##//dkon pa ni ྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾
4a2(rgyud): dag la rab tu shis//khen khor yug shing ldan pa'i//sa gzhi yon tan kun ldan pa'i//'khor ྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾ dag na chu yod pa//dkyil 'khor dag la sa 
4a3(rgyud): de shing//zhi ba'i yas la sa gzhi ni//྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾ dka' ba dag ni rtag par bya//rgyas ྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾྾
4a4(rgyud): spyod pa nag por shis//gangs chu'i ngos la pad ma yi//mtsho dang brgya mtsho'i nye 'khor la//chu'i gling bzhi nyams dga' ba//zhi 
4a5(rgyud): ba'i dkyil 'khor bri bar bya//ma mo'i gnas dang khang stong dang//dben pa'i gnas dang dud khrod dang //nyams mi dga' ba'i 
4b1(rgyud): yul gang yin//de dag tu ni mngon spyod bri//ri rtse dang ni ba lang lhas //mchod rten sku gzugs khang pa dang//de la sogs pa'i phyogs 
4b2(rgyud): dag tu//rgyas pa yi dkyil 'khor bri//mchod rten chen po nyams dga' dang//dben pa gnas dag dang//ri'i rtse mo brgya
4b3(rgyud): chen gyi //dngos grub dkyil 'khor bri bar bya//ri dag dang ni ri bo dang//ri'i zom dang phug dang//lha min phug dang 
4b4(rgyud): 'dod pa yis //sa 'og rdo rje'i dkyil 'khor bri//pad ma kha ni bye ba'i tshal//dang skya dag ni sgra sgrogs pa//rgyal srid sa 'am nor 'dod
4b5(rgyud): pas//dpal gyi dkyil 'khor thaMd bri//ma mo'i gnas dang ri mo dang//lha rnams kyi ni gnas dag tu//gdon dbab pa dang 'jom(sa) byed pa//rdo rje lcags kyu'i 
4b6(rgyud): dkyil 'khor bri//lam po che mo bzhi mdo dang//mchod rten phyag na rdo rje   yi//mdun du bsgrub pa po yis ni//khro rgyal dkyil 'khor rgyal
5a1(rgyud): @##// O //'jaM pas dbyiIII gshegs pa'i dkyil 'khor ni//gnas ni bzang po gzhan du bzang//mkhas pa yis ni khyad par du//thams cad bri bar bya ba yin//
5a2(rgyud): rab tu gnas sogs kun//mkhas pa las ni khyad par du//pad ma'i mtsho 'i ne 'khor du//cho ga bzhin du bri bar bya//ri'i rtse mor mkhas pa yis//med las byung ba'i cho ga bzhun//rnam
5a3(rgyud): pa kun rtsogs dkyil 'khor du//khyad par du ni bri bar bya//rnam par nges pa'i gnas 'di ni//khyad par du ni bsten par bya//rab tu sgrims te thams cad la //sa ni rnam gsum yongasu rtag//
5a4(rgyud): khyad par phun sum ma ldan na//phun sum tshogs pa kun bzang ba'i//tshogs ba dag gi rnyed par dka'//mdor na so bzhi mnyam pa dang //mdangs sam shar du gzhol ba bzhed//
5a5(rgyud): chu mang ldan pa'i gnas dag ni//shing ljon bzang po/rgyas pa dang//bdag gi dmig yid 'gu ba dang//mtshe med zug rngu spangs pa ste//dkyil 'khor gnas su shes pa yin//
5a6(rgyud): mi yid chos grub kun thab nas//drang srong gnas dang (ba) blang gnas//phug dang ri'i rtse mo dang//sa bzhi gang na gnas pa dang//khang steng dang ni rdo leb dang//mchod rten drung dang 'bab chu'i kling
5a7(rgyud): mtsho rnams kyi ni 'gram dag tu//sa bzhi sbyang dang rtag pa dang//nan tan du ni bya mi dgos//'thas par phyag dang de mi dgos//mthon dman laswogs skyon rnams kyang //der ni dgos pa
5a8(rgyud): bskyed mi dgos//rang bzhin gnas pa'i sa bzhi la//byi dor legs par bas nas su//legs pas bran bzhin gsang sngags bzlas//de'i sbyang bas de bzhin no//zug rngu'i nyes pa thams
5b1(rgyud): cad ni//gal te 'byung bar mi nus so//der ni gsang sngags kho na yis//sbyang ba legs par byas 'gyur na//brtan par gyur pa'i las rnams ni//gdon rnams tshar gcod la sogs dang //drag
5b2(rgyud): shul spyod pa dag la yang //sa ni rtag par bya mi dgos//dkyil 'khor sa bzhi cho ga la//khro bo ki li ki la yis//ba'i rnam lnga'i la bzlas pas/gsang rtor byas nas sbyang bar bya//gzhan gyis mi thub
5b3(rgyud): la sogs pas/rgyal ba gtsug tor nyid kyis sbyang //phyag na pad ma'i dkyil 'khor kun//sa bzhi sbyang bar bya ba ni//zhi ba 'dren pa'i zhes bya ba'i//dpal mos cho ga bzhin du basbyang //phyag na rdo
5b4(rgyud): rje'i dkyil 'khor rnams//khyad par du ni khro bos sbyang //las su bya ba thams cad kyang //cho ga bzhin 'di yis bya//rgyal ba'i gtso dang zhi bas 'dren//kho bo stobs chen zhes bya ba//
5b5(rgyud): 'di gsum rigs kyi sum char gyis//las rnams kun la sbyang bya ste//sa sbyang ba dang bsrung ba dang /slob ma sta gon gnas pa dang /bgegs rnams gsal dang dra dag dang
5b6(rgyud): me tog mngon par sngags pa dang //de la sogs pa'i las kun la//sgrims te sbyar bar bya ba ni//so so'i rgyang las bya ba ni//bsang sngags rab tu sgrags pa gang //ston zla'i brang po'i nya nas ni//
5b7(rgyud): dpyid zla tha chungs nya'i bar//'di yi bar du mkhas pa yis//dkyil 'khor cho ga bzhin du bri//bgegs kyi dkyil 'khor nad can dang //de bzhin bsrung ba'i dkyil 'khor dag//spyang mi dgos par
5b8(rgyud): dbyar yang ni//mkhas pas dus bzhin bri bar bya//slob ma dbang skur ched du ni//dkyil 'khor yongs su skrag gang dang //gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor gang yin dang //dam tshig dkyil 'khor
6a1(rgyud): @#//gang yin dang//nor skab pa'i gang yin dang//'byor pa ched dang sgrub pa dang//chen po'i dkyil 'khor gang yin dang //da dag thams cad mkhas pa yis//zla ba bdun po dag la bya//yul 
6a2(rgyud): dang dus dang slob ma dang//bdag med yin tan shes bya ste//mkhas pas dbyar yang dkyil 'khor gyi//las kyi cho ga bzhin du bri//dri dang me tog bdug spos dang //lha bshos sbyin srog las kyi rnams//
6a3(rgyud): 'phongs par gyur kyang mi bri ste//bris nas nyes par 'chi bar 'gyur//tshes grangs don dang dus min dang//gnas chen cho ga mi ldan par//mkhas pas cho ga mi bri'o//bris na dgos pa med par 
6a4(rgyud): 'chi//mkhas pas ji skad bshad pa yi//zla bar yar gyi de dag la//zla ba nyi ma bcu gsum la/tshes brgyad dag gam bcu bzhi 'am/tshes bcu dang ni bcu gcig gam//
6a5(rgyud): yang na yar gyi tshes gcig gam//tshes lnga 'am ni tshes bdun nam/yang na ches grangs gsum yang rung//nyi ma bcu po 'di dag la dkyil 'khor rab tu sgrims te bri//
6a6(rgyud): mar gyi do'i dus 'tshams la'ang//dkyil 'khor bri ba 'gal ba med//tshes pa lnga 'am/tshes bdun nam/zla ba nya la khyad par du/phyag na pad ma'i dkyil 'khor bri//cho ga rnams ni bya bar
6a7(rgyud): shes//sa bzhi 'og gi dkyil 'khor dang//drag shul gyi ni spyod pha rnams//khro bo rnams kyi dkyil 'khor gang//de dag mar ngo la yang bri//zla ba nya la khyad par du/rgyal ba'i dkyil 'khor bri 
6a8(rgyud): bar shegs/////////spyid zla 'bring dang tha chungs la//mkhas pas dkyil 'khor chen po bri//gang tshe yo byad ldan pa dang//de'i tshe yang dkyil 'khor bri//gang tshe slob ma'i snyen bkur gyis//
6b1(rgyud): bla na dag ni 'gu 'gyur ba//de'i tshe de nang gnas 'gyur pa//dbang skur ched du dkyil 'khor bri//nyi ma zla ba bzas zin dang //ya mtshan che ltas byung ba dang //phyo 'phrul gyi ni zla ba la//sgrims te
6b2(rgyud): dkyil 'khor bri bar bya//snga gro rgyas dang bskyod pa dang //nyi ma gung la drag shul dang //dgod kyang zhi ba'i blo can gyis//cho ga bzhin dkyil 'khor bri//dkyil 'khor dgi na ma lus pa//nyi ma ri rtse nub nas
6b3(rgyud): nyi ma shar bar ma gyur par//mchod nas gshegasu gsol bar bgyi//khyad par gyi yang cho ga yang //lha dus bya ba'i dus bzhi bshad//gzhan du cho ga nyams pa'i phyir//dngos grub 'byung bar mi
6b4(rgyud): 'gyur ro//phyi'i dkyil 'khor blo ldan pas//nam yang nyin par mi bri'o//nyin bar bris na de dang ni//sdug bsngal nyamasu myong bar 'gyur//dgongs ka mgo btsugs nas phyed/
6b5(rgyud): nas//drag shul spyod par 'gyur ba yin//mtshan ma dus las 'das pa na//nye ba mang po 'byung bar 'gyur//mtshan ma rab tu n-i ba'i phyir//spyir n-i 'dod pa bya ba'i phyir//dam tshig
6b6(rgyud): dag la sogs pa'i las//de lta bas na mtshan mo rtsam//nyi ma nub nas lha rnams ni//nyes par byin gyis rlab par 'du//gzhan dag gi ni khyad par du//byin gyis rlob pa byed par 'gyur//cho gas
6b7(rgyud): spyan drangs gsang sngags dang //lha yang nyi ma nub nas/phan par bzhed pas dkyil 'khor du//nges par byin gyis rlob par mdzod//yang dag bzhi ba'i sems kyis ni//dus dang tshes grangs
6b8(rgyud): bzh-i ba dang /sngags kyi spyan 'dren byed pa ni//bsam pa'-i dngos grub thob par 'gyur//phur pa dang ni zla ba dang /pa wa sangs dang nyi ma dang /gzhan la rab tu drag pa'o//las ni rab tu rtsam par bya
7a1(rgyud): @#//rgyal ba dang skrag dangs ka dang //me bzhi nag pa tha skar dang //sgra /bsogs pa sa ka ni//dkyil 'khor dag la rab tu shis//de la rtsol bzhi (ba)dang /rgyu skar dag ni bzang po la //
7a2(rgyud): blo dang ldan pas dkyil 'khor dri//'bebs ldan rgyal rgyas byed pa dang //gsal dang mar ldan nor can dang //bzang po chu la tshangs byed dang //de dang bkra shis khyab 'jug dang //skyes pa'i bdag dang 
7a3(rgyud): tha skar byams//tshig bzangs pa wa sangs pa'o//yud tsam rnams ni rgyud kun gyi//bya ba'i cho ga rnams la shis//de dag la sogs bzang po dang //byed pa bzang la rtag tu bri//mi bzang ba la rtson pa'i
7a4(rgyud): las//gang du yang ni mi 'grub bo//de ltar bas na yud tsam dang /chos grangs rgyu skar byed pa dang //rmi lam bzang po dag la las brtsams te//bzang po min pa'i las rnams dang 
7a5(rgyud): bzang po nyid la brtsams par bya//ltas ni bzang dang mi bzang bas//grub pa dang ni mi 'grub ston//de lta bas na brtsam pa dag//mtshan ma rab tu 'bad te rtag//de yang gzhan 
7a6(rgyud): yang sa dag ni//sbyang bar bya bar rab tu bshad//zhag bdun gyi ba ma du logasu//ci ltar rig pas bsrung bya ste//'jor gyis sa ni rkos byas shing //de nas zug rngu 'byung bar bya//las byas
7a7(rgyud): sa bdag 'grub mi 'gyur//zug rngu nyes pa rus pa dang //gyo mo dang ni thal ba dang //sdom dum dang ni tshe ma dang //rab tu sgrims te ma lus 'byung //de nas tho bas bong ba dag//bkrum nas
7a8(rgyud): zhib mor byas te bcag//de nas pa'i lan gyis ni//yang nas yang du gdab ba zhing //lag 'thil bar da mnyam byas nas//ba'i lja baM chur sbyangs bas//shar gyi phyogs nas mgo 
7b1(rgyud): btsugs nas//blo dang ldan pas legs pas byugs//de nas de'i dbus su ni//khong du chud zhig rkos nas der//'bru rnams dang ni rin chen kun//sngags kyis btab ste bzhug par bya//
7b2(rgyud): de ltar byas shing dbyig dag gis//dbyig dang ldan par byas ni sa ni//blo dang ldan pas ci rigs par//sa bzhi yongasu bzung bar byas//yang dag sdams pas zhag gsum nas//las kun byed pa'i
7b3(rgyud): gsang sngags dang//gang gi dkyil 'khor gang yin pa//de dag gis ni yongs bzung bya//gcig gis dri yis chu yis ni//yongasu gsang rtor bya ba ste//gcig bshos kyi ni lag pa yis
7b4(rgyud): sa la bris te mngon par sngags//blo yis yongs su bzung byas nas//gnas ni yongasu bzung ba yin//las kun byed pa'i gsang sngags kyis//de nas slob ma yongasu
7b5(rgyud): bzung //bu mo gzhon nus bka' ba'i skud//blangs nas bzhi bcur rtsa dgur bzlas//slob ma rnams ni blos bsams nas//mnyam par gzhag te 'dud pa bor//
7b6(rgyud): de nas slob ma yongs bzung nas//las ni gyegs ni med par 'gyur//gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor bri ba'i tshe//do nub lta bu sta gon gnas//thog ma nyi ma shar ba'i tshe//sa phyogs kyi ni
7b7(rgyud): sa bdag rtag//de nas dkyil 'khor bkod pa dag//yid kyis rnam par yongasu rtag//sngags rgyud dag las gyur pa'i zas//bzang la gtsang zhing snum bag can//rjesu 'thun zhing yid 'ong ba
7b8(rgyud): slob mar bcas par ran par bzla//de nas nub gur khrus bas shing //rtsang la gos ni rab tu dkar po dga'o//bla ma slob ma dang bcas par//dkyil 'khor bsrung du gdong bar bya//dkyil 'khor bri
8a1(rgyud): @#//ba'i sa bzhi ni//byi dor legs byas logs byung ba'i//khro bo chen po bzlas pa yi//spos chus gsang bar byas pa la//dbus su sta mgon gnas pa dag//rigs rnams kyi ni 
8a2(rgyud): snying pos bya//dkyil 'khor bdag po'i rigs sngags kyis//las rnams thams cad bya bar bshad//sa bzhi'i steng du byug spos kyis//dkyil 'khor sor grangs bcu gnyis bya//dkyil 'khor gyi ni 
8a3(rgyud): rigs sngags kyis//de nas gtso bo'i las rnams ni//mi gi mdo la bsams nas su//byug spos me tog la sogs pas//snying po ci rigs mchod par bya//lha bshos dag kyang dbul bar bya :rigs rnams
8a4(rgyud): spos mngon par sngags pas phul//de nas tsan dan sbyar ba yi//chu la mngon par sngags bya zhing //de la me tog gis gtor nas//cho ga bzhin du bdug pas bdug//so shing u dum b+ha ra dang
8a5(rgyud): a shad di na rma med pa//ha cang mi sbom mi 'phra zhing //sor grangs bcu gnyis tshad du bya//spos kyi chus ni legs par bkrus//skud pa dag gis legs par bkris//spos
8a6(rgyud): kyis byugs shing bdug pas bdug//lag pas bran zhing rim pas ni//snying pos lan grangs mang po 'am//yang na lan bdun bzlas brjod bya//slob ma rnams kyi grangs las ni//so shing la sogs
8a7(rgyud): bsogs par bya//so shing mgo zhabs snyams byas la//thor to gcig gis mgo byas nas//rme mo dag ni bca' bar bya//de nas mkhas pas gnas dang ni//bdag dang slob ma rtan srungs nas//lha
8a8(rgyud): rnams thams cad legs mchod dang //dang po bgegs ni zh-i bya'-i phyir//mar la sbyar ba'i til dag dang //mar gyi sreg lus sbyin sreg bya//'bras chen zho sbyar sbyin sreg bya//de'i 'og tu 
8b1(rgyud): snying po ste//de nas zhi ba'i sbyin sreg ni//zhi ba'i sbyin sreg nyid kyis bya//de nas slob ma legs rtag ste//sta gon gnas pa tshul bzhin bya//slob ma rigs su btsun gtsang sgra can//snying stobs che zhing
8b2(rgyud): chos lhur len//dad pa che ni zhing bzod dang ldan//dran dang ldan zhing mkhas pa ste//drang rkan che la gdong rtsol can//rtan zhing dul ba snyan par smra//smra mkhas pa la blo bskyed can//yang dag shes shing byas
8b3(rgyud): la gzo//blo dang ldan zhing spobs pa che//chen por 'dod cing nga rgyal med//'dog dang gzugs dang na tshod bzang //stan chos dag la goms par bya//ha cang mi ring mi thung ste//de la sogs pa'i yon tan dang
8b4(rgyud): ldan pa'i blo ldan slob mar shis//rang bzhin ngan rtum g.yo can dang //snying rje med cing ngan par 'tsho//yan lag nyams shing yan lag med//ha cang sbom dang skyem dang 'dar//mig dmar
8b5(rgyud): 'dog ni khro gnyer can//kha dog mdzes shing yan lag rnams//mtshan dang ldan pa mi yan dang //snying rje med cang de 'dra'i slob ma skyon mang ba//slob dpon dag gis spang bar bya//sha tshug+sa
8b6(rgyud): ma legs yon tan med//rigs ni dma' zhing ngan par 'tsho//nad bu can dang dad med pa'i//slob ma rnams ni yongasu spang //bdag nyid ngan pa'i mi gang dang //smad 'tshong ma la rab tu btud zhing //
8b7(rgyud): chang dad can dang rgyan la sred//de dag rtag tu yongasu spang //dkon mchog gsum la sems dad cing //tshul khrims kyi ni sdom pas stsams//theg pa che la mos pa yis//slob ma yongasu bzung bar
8b8(rgyud): shis//slob ma yan lag ma nyams dang //lhag par yon tan ldan pa dang //nad med rigs ni btsun pa dang //dad cing rtul zhugs rtan pa dkon//dkon mchog gsum la gang dad cing //
9a1(rgyud): @#//theg pa chen po'i lugs la dga'//bsod nams 'dod pa'i sems can ni//chen po de bdag mdor bzung ngo//bde ba gshegs gasngang sngags sgrub pa la;don gnyer brtson pa'i
9a2(rgyud): slob ma ni;gzugs ngan yon tan mi ldan yang//blo dang ldan pas bzhug par bya //dkyil 'khor cho ga 'khor bzhi pa//theg pa che la dga' ba'i bdag//rang gi slab pa la brtson pa //kun gyi che ga
9a3(rgyud): yin par bzhed //kha cig gsang sngags sgrub pa'i phyir//de las gzhan pa'i bsod nams 'dod//gzhan dag 'jig rten pha rol don//'di ni dkyil 'khor 'jug par byed//'jig rten pha rol yin
9a4(rgyud): par ni//dad pa chen po bskyed byas nas//blo ldan dkyil 'khor 'jug bya'i phyir//tshe 'di'i 'bras bu 'dod mi bya//'jig rten pha rol don du ni//skyes bu 'di'i 'bras rgyas pa//de ltar tshe
9a5(rgyud): 'di 'dod pa yi//'jig rten pha rol rgyas mi 'gyur////mkhas pas slob ma gcig gam gsum//lnga 'am yang na bdun dag gam//nyi shu dag ni lnga'i bar//zung du ma gyur
9a6(rgyud): yongasu bzung//de las lhag pa'i slob ma dag//yongasu bzung bar mi shis so//mdor na slob ma phan tshun du//nang mi gshin pa mi bzung ngo//gcig la gcig ni sems bzang zhing//zhi dul bla ma
9a7(rgyud): la phan pa //'phags pa'i yul dang rigs skyes pa'i//slob ma mdor na bzung bar bya//de ltar sreg par bya ba yis//zas ni za gcig za ba yis//de'i nub mo ci nus par//de'i sdom pa 'dzin du bcud
9a8(rgyud): rtsang mi gos dkar dgos pa yis//slob ma mdor na dag ni//shar phyogs kha bltas 'khod pa la//sta gon gnas pa rtsam par bya//dang po gsum la skyabasu btang//de nas bla mas skyabs chub seMs//
9b1(rgyud): ma skyed pa ni skyed par bya//bskyed pa dag kyang dran par bya//de nas khro bo zlas pa yis//spos chus mgo la gtor bya zhing //mgo bo grags te mnyam gzhag nas//lan bdun du yang bzlas
9b2(rgyud): po rjod bya//byug spos dag gis skus pa yis//lags pa mgo la gzhag bzhin du//mkhas pas rig pa'i rgyal po yis//snying po yod ni lan bdun bzlas//rgyal ba'i rigs la rigs sngags
9b3(rgyud): rgyal//'khor lo bsgyur ba'i yi ge gcig//pad ma'i rigs la rigs sngags rgyal //rta 'grin yi ge bcu pa'o//rdo rje'i rigs las rigs sngags rgyal//de bzhin gnod mdzes mthu bo 
9b4(rgyud): che//yi ge hU~M ni bzhi dang ldan//las rnams kun la rab tu grags//khro bo bdud rtsi thabs sbyor cing //rigs gsum dag gi spyi la yang //bgegs rnams thams
9b5(rgyud): cad 'joms pa'i phyir//gsang ba yaM ni bdag pos bshad//de nas las kun byed pa yi//sngags kyis spyi bo bram gyis zlas//de nyid kyis ni gsang rtor bya//bdug spos
9b6(rgyud): dag ni rab tu bdug//dbang skur ba'i bum pa ni//'bru la sogs pa kun ldan pa//chu ni cung zad blugs nas su//rig pa'i rgyal po mngon par bsngags pa'i//spos chus mchod yon phul nas
9b7(rgyud): ni//cho ga bzhin du sta gon bya//me tog dag kyang blugs nas su//bdug spos kyis ni bdug par bya//byi de nyin mo dus gsum du//mkhas pas bzlas pa legs par bya//dkyil 'khor du ni 
9b8(rgyud): bzlas nas su//de yis mngon par dbang bskur bya//slob ma bdun bzhi tshogs pa rnams//shar gyi phyogasu kha bltas shing //shar gyi phyogasu kha bltas//thal mo sbyar te 'khod pa las//
10a1(rgyud): @#//so shing go rims bzhin du byin//slob dpon shar du kha ltas te//mi smra bar song shing bca'//bza' ba zin nas mi dgas shing //phan tshun du yang mi 'dor ro//so shing 
10a2(rgyud): mdun du drang por dor//de tshe bdag la blta bar bya//rtse mo gyen du 'greng gyur na//de ni dngos grub mchog kyang bya//de bzhin shar du kha bltas na//de ni dngos grub 'gring du bshad//
10a3(rgyud): mtshams dang byang du kha bltas//de tshe bdag la blta par bya//'jig rten pa'-i dngos grub thob//so shing bor ba brgya zhig la//gal te 'thur du kha ltas na//de yis nges par sa 'og gi//
10a4(rgyud): dngos grub de la the tsom med//slob mas me reg byas nas ni//snga ma bzhin du 'khod pa dang //re re la yang gdung ba'i phyir//bla mas las kun byed pa'o//gsang sngags dag ni spos 
10a5(rgyud): kyi chu//skur bas blugs nas lan bdun klud//der ni 'thungs nas ni//phyi rol du ni kha klu bya//de nas mchod pa yang byas nas//bdug spos lag tu thogs nas su//blo gros 
10a6(rgyud): can gyis lha rnams la//gus par gsol ba btab par bya//gang gi dkyil 'khor yin pa'i sngags//de la thog mar gsol btab//go rims de'i tshul gyis ni//lha rnams kun gyis btab p+Ra
10a7(rgyud): gsol//'di zhes bya ba'i bcom ldan 'das//rig sngags rgyal la phyag 'tshal lo//bdag nyid snying rtser gyur nas ni//slob ma rnams la rtse ba dang//khyed rnams la n-i mchod pa'i phyir//sang ni 
10a8(rgyud): dkyil 'khor bri bar bya//bcom ldan bdag ni gus pa la//de bas gnang bar mdzad pa rigs//sangs rgyas 'jig rten mgon rtse dang//byang chub sems dpa' dgra bcom 'dad//gsang sngags lha 
10b1(rgyud): gzhan gang lags dang //'jig rten skyong ba'i lha rnams dang//gang yang 'byung po mthu chen dang//gang su rdo rje lha'i spyan mnga' ba'i//sems can rnams la stan la mngon dga' rnams//bdag la dgong+sa
10b2(rgyud): par mdzad du gsol//bdag ming 'di zhes bgyi ba yis//sa 'dir dkyil 'khor bzang po'i ming //'di zhes bya ba ci nus kyis//cho ga bzhin du bris lags na//bdag dang slob mar bcas pa la //
10b3(rgyud): thugs ni rtse bar dgongs nasu//tshogs pa thams cad dkyil 'khor du//byin gyis rlob par mdzad du gsol//de ltar lan gsum smras nas ni//bsam pa dag pas phyag byas te//stod pa'i 
10b4(rgyud): tshig ni brjod pas su//de nas slar du gshegasu gsol//'dod chags bral dang ldan pa'i chos//slob ma rnams la bshad nas su//ku sha bting ba'i sa gzhi la//shar phyogs 
10b5(rgyud): sngas ltas nyal du bzhug//de nas nang par nam langs nas//rmi lam ci mthong bri bar bya//thos nas bzang ngam mi bzang ngan//the tsom med par bshad par bya//
10b6(rgyud): rmi lam sangs rgyas mthong na ni//yon tan rgya mtsho mngon sum zhing//mchod rten dang ni sku gzugs mthong//de la mchod pa brtson pa dang//dge slong dag la chos nyan nam//yang na gzhan
10b7(rgyud): la nyan yang rung//chos ni rnam par spyod pa dang//de bzhin glegs bam klog pa dang//rmi lam dge 'dun mthong ba dang//yang na dge slong gcig pu mthong//de dag 'grogs shing rnam zer dang
10b8(rgyud): yang na dge slong dngos por 'gyur//gtsug lag khang dag mthong ba dang//de bzhin dge slong ma dag mthong//rgyal ba'i sras dag mthong ba dang//de bzhin pha ma bla ma dang//mdza'o rnam 
11a1(rgyud): @#//rnam pa sna tshogs rnams//rmi lam na ni mthong ba shis//rig sngags dang ni dngos grub thob//de bzhin slob ma 'dzin pa dang//kun dga' rnam pa sna tshogs mthong//
11a2(rgyud): 'bab chu rnam pa sna tshogs dang//ri dang blang po che dag dang//mthong na yang ni bzang por grags//rgyal po dag ni mthong na shis//bde gshegs spyod la cha 'thun pa'i//rka thub can dang bram
11a3(rgyud): ze dang//byug po skyes bu dam pa dang//ba dang rta dang ngang pa dang//seng ge dang ni ri dgas dang//rmi lam du ni mthong na shis//bkra shis pa'i bya rnams dang//gser bdag gi ni gter
11a4(rgyud): rnams dang//rin po che'i dang rdul dag dang//nor ni rnam pa sna tshogs dang/mdor na 'byung khungs rnam pa dang//mtshon cha rnams dang 'bru rnams dang//'bras bu rnams dang me
11a5(rgyud): tog dang//rgyan ni rnam pa tshogs dang//kha bzas dang ni brgyan rnams dang//byis pa dang ni gzhon nu dang//bu med gzugs ni bzang mo dang//rmi lam du ni ma thong na
11a6(rgyud): bzang//yang na thob cing rigs smra dang//rmi lam du ni dbang bskur dang//rgyal por 'gyur ba'i sbyor ba dang//dmag dpon du ni 'gyur ba dang//g.yul las rgyal dang dgra gsod dang//
11a7(rgyud): gnyen dang lhan gcig 'grogs pa dang//lha rnams dag ni mthong ba dang//theg pa dang ni shing rta zhon//de bzhin ri dang khang thog 'dzeg//ngo 'tshal rnam pa sna tshogs dag//rmi lam
11a8(rgyud): du ni mthong na dge//yam shing me la sbyin byed//bya ba bzang po dag la 'jug//chu las rgal dang ro las rgal//rkun po dang ni tshar gtsod dang stobs 'gyed pa dag rgyal ba dang//gya stobs
11b1(rgyud): rdob cing rgod pa dang//rtse mo sna tshogs rmi ba dang//de bzhin rtsal gzungs byed pa dang//bkra shis dag ni thams cad kyang//mthong bar rab tu dge bar bshad //gsang sngags chos gzhan pa dang//
11b2(rgyud): dga' ston dge ba mthong ba dang//sgrub pa bzang po brtson dang//de la sogs pa'i bltas rnams ni//dge ba rnam pa du ma dag//blo y-is rnam par spyad bya zhing//de las zlog pa spang bar bya//dge ba rnams ni
11b3(rgyud): grub par 'gyur//mi dge rnams ni mi 'grub po//mi dge ba dag shes nas su//de dag mkhas pas spang bar bya//dag ba yi ni las rnams ni//rab dang 'bring dang tha dang//gsang sngags dngos grub
11b4(rgyud): rnaMs gsum pa//rab dang 'bring dang ma grub //mi dge ba la bzhi ba yi//sbyin sreg zhi ba'i gsang sngags kyis//ba'i mar la brgya rtsa brgyad //cho ga bzhin du zhi bar bar bya//
11b5(rgyud): de nas slob ma'i sdig bral zhing //rnam par dag pa de dag la //rjes su rtse bar bya ba'i phyir //ci 'dod par ni bzung bar bya//dkyil 'khor gyi ni sngags rnams kun
11b6(rgyud): gnang ba rjesu dran byas nas//gos dkar dgo zhing ma yengs par//dkyil 'khor gyi ni drung du dgod//las kun byed pa'-i gsang sngags kyis//thog ma nyid du gsang rtor bya//mtshon sna lnga'i
11b7(rgyud): srad bu la //de nyid kyi ni brgyar bzlas te//bzang po'-i bltas dang sbyar nas ni//mkhas pas dkyil 'khor thig gdab po//thig skud kyi ni mtshon sna lnga//dkar dang dmar dang de bzhin ser//
11b8(rgyud): sngo dang ljang nag po ste/mtshon rtsi rnams kyang de dang 'dra//dkon mchog gsum la mchod bya zhing /lha rnams la yang phyag byas nas//mkhas pas srad bu bri bar bya//nang par nyi ma shar ba'i tshe//
12a1(rgyud): @#//blo yis phyogs cha legs rtags la//dang por Inas mnyam ste bzung//slob dpon byang du khaltas te//de nas g.yas phyogs bskor nas ni//bdag nyid nub phyogs kha blta Ite//
12a2(rgyud): de nas srang bu lo phyegasu//bdag nyid gyis ni bri bar bya//de'i 'og tu byang Iphyogasu//mkhas pas srad bu bri bar bya//dang por bdag nyid ma phyogasu//'dug nas phyogs gnyis rtag par bya//
12a3(rgyud): de'i 'og tu Iphyogs dang //phyogs gnyis nas ni srad bu bri//bden bral dbang ldan dgra gnyis nas//srad bu 'dzin pa lta bar bya//gru bzhi mnyam par lham nasu//rgya ram du ni srad bu bri//rgya 
12a4(rgyud): Ithams cad mnyam par bsam//de ltar srad bu//rten la phab//snyams nas de'i dkyil du ni//phur bu gcig tu gdab par/bya//de nas de'i 'og tu yang//grwa bzhir phur pu bzhi btab
12a5(rgyud): po//de bzhin dkyil 'khor gnyis dag kyang//gsum par legs par grwa dag dang//che chung dag kyang rim pa bzhin//yongasu brtsam par bya bar bshad//de las gzhan pa 'khor gyi ni//
12a6(rgyud): dkyil 'khor bshad pa gang yin dang//de kun phye ma dkar po yis//cig rkyang du ni bri bar bya//dkyil 'khor nyi rim sum rim 'am//bzhi 'am mang po byas pa la//phyi rol gang yin de las ni//
12a7(rgyud): sgo cig pa dang rgya cher bya//gzhan ni phyogs kyi cha bzhin du//sgo bzhin sgo khyud bcas par bya//yang na thams cad dgos med par//sgo re re zhig bya ba yin//snying po'i dkyil 'khor nges par ni//
12a8(rgyud): rab tu bzang po sgo bzhir bya//nub phyogs sgo 'am yang na ni//gang la ni ji skad bshad pa bzhin//de dag rnams ni thams cad kyi//rnam par dbye ba yin par grags//lha mo'i gtso bor gyur pa rnaMs/
12b1(rgyud): rtag tu dkyil 'khor bkod par bya//de'i lhag ma'i dkyil 'khor ni//gnyis pa dag tu dgod par bya//'jig rten skyong ba la sogs pa//dkyil 'khor gsum par bsdu bar bya//de las gzhan rnaMs
12b2(rgyud): ji skad du//bshad pa de bzhin kho nar dgod//dkyil 'khor thig bdab thig skud ni//kha sgyur gtsang ma 'phra zhing sra//de bzhin bu mo gzhon nus bkal//gsar pa shin tu rtsang ma dang//mdzes shing
12b3(rgyud): shin tu ran pa dang//dri zhim reg na bde ba dang//legs par bkal dang 'phra ba dang//sbom 'phra laswogs bkal legs dang//mjur mdud med par gyur pa yi//thig skud metog spos mchod pas//
12b4(rgyud): shes rab can gyis thig bdab po//mchod cig shing gi phur bu dang//mthur du rtse bstan mngon sngags pa//dkyil 'khor bris pa'-i cho ga la//phyogs bzhi dag tu bdab par bya//ltas ngan
12b5(rgyud): byung na dngos grub //srad bu chad na bla ma 'chi//ring thung khyer na nad kyis 'debs//phyogs 'khrugs bla ma mos par 'gyur//de bas thams cad nan tan tu//mkhas 
12b6(rgyud): pas legs par thag btab pa//khyim pa'i dpyad la ci 'byung ba'i//sa'i nges pa de dang 'dra//de nas bla ma'-i ci 'byor ba'i//'phags pa dge 'dun chos ston gsol//yang na bla mas skos pa yi//
12b7(rgyud): slob ma rnams kyis chos gsol//gal te mi 'byor dge 'dun gyis//bskos pa'i dge 'dun chos ston gsol //yang na sangs rgyas mchod pa 'am//dge 'dun dag la yon yang dbul//
12b8(rgyud): bla mas slob ma dang bcas pas//khro bo'i rgyal po rjes dran zhing //skyur ru laswogs pA//phyi 'phred tshul bzhin khrus rtsang bya//gos dkar po ni yang dag dgos//las byas rnaM pa kun dang ldan//
13a1(rgyud): @#//byams pa'i sems ni nyer gzhag nas//dkyil 'khor gyi ni drung du 'ongs//bla ma tshogs rnams kun bsags shing //lta ba ngan pa thams cad spangs//dkyil 'khor bri ba thaMd la//
13a2(rgyud): bla mas mkhas pa rtag tu bya//dang po dkyil 'khor sa bzhi la//ba'i gcin dang sres pa yi//ba'i lji ba skus byas shing //de nas spos chus gsang rdor bya//sa bzhi kho ra khor yug tu //phyag phyag
13a3(rgyud): bdab cing phyag bdar bya//yid bzhin mos par bya ba'i phyir//metog sil ma gtor bar bya//srad bu dag gis yongasu bskor//rgyal mtshan rta babs dag gis brgyan //rgyus pa'i phreng bas
13a4(rgyud): rnam bre zhing //bkra shis cho ga star par bya//de nas byang gi phyogs gcig tu//yo byad thams cad bsogs par bya//khro bo'i rgyal pos srungs nas ni//blo dang ldan pas dgod par bya//
13a5(rgyud): gnas ni gsang rdor bya ba dang //bgegs rnaMs gsal bar byas nas ni//bdag dang dkyil 'khor dag dang ni//dang por kun la bsrung bya ste//bkra shis lhas dang sbyar nas ni//
13a6(rgyud): mos pa'i sems ni bskyed bya zhing //'tshams kyi dus su bab pa yi//srod kyi dus la rtsol bar bya//ltas dang mi ldan gyur na yang //sems ni zhum par mi bya'o//rig gsum dag la 
13a7(rgyud): chags byas nas//dal gyis legs par brtsam par bya//ltas ngan dag gis yang dang yang //rtud par byung bar gyur nas ni //de la dngos grub med gyur gyis//de tshe rtsam par mi bya'o//
13a8(rgyud): yang na de ni zhi ba'i phyir//yam shing me ni sbal ba la//ba'i mar la brgya rtsa brgyad//zhi ba yi ni sbyin sreg bya//de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs la ni//sangs rgyas spyan gyis zhi bar bya//pad ma'i
13b1(rgyud): rigs la grags ldan pa//rdo rje'i rigs la ma ma ki//rigs gsum gyi ni spyi la yang//rigasngags chen mo ma ma ki//blo dang ldan pas las kun la//de bas de ni zhi bar bya//dkyil 
13b2(rgyud): 'khor bdag po cho ga bzhin//de bas mchod yon dbul bar bya//snod ni gser ram dngul dag gam//zangs la byas pa 'am dung yang rung//shing la sogs pa rta dag gam//khang pa rtsang ma 
13b3(rgyud): dag kyang rung//der ni me tog dkar po blugs//bdug spos kyis kyang bdug par bya//de nas dri zhim chu blugs nas//blo ldan lag gis dran byas shing//bdug spos kyis ni bdug pa 
13b4(rgyud): bzhin du//yid kyis rab tu bzlas brjod byaHde nas blo dang ldan pa yis//de nas rtsa ba'i gsang sngags zlas byed cingaHbus mo gnyis btsugs mchod yon dbul//yang na dkyil 'khor
13b5(rgyud): thams cad kyi//las la mchod yon don du ni//me tog bdug spos dkar po ni//bzang po dri zhim byug spos ni//bdug spos dug du byug spos dang//srog chags yan lag bcas
13b6(rgyud): pa dang//lus kyi bya ba thams cad la//chu ni gtsang mar btsag par bya//dkyil 'khor dag tu gcig skyes pa'i//byug spos dag gam bdug spos dag//me tog skam la skyes pa ni//dri zhim pa
13b7(rgyud): dang dkar po shis//de nas slob ma swog nas ni//spos kyi chu yis gsang rtor bya//tshogs bsrung bar byas pa rnams//phyogs gcig tu yang bkod nas ni//shes pha rol phyin
13b8(rgyud): blags ste//gus pas yang ni phyag byas nas//'phags pa'i tshogs kun bsogs nas ni//de nas dkyil 'khor bri bar bya//mkhas pas lcags ni rnam lnga 'am//phye ma
14a1(rgyud): @#//dkar la swogs pa//dad cing yon tan ldan pa yi//slob mas rin chen phye mas bris//rin chen lcags rnams ma 'byor na//sa lu btags pa'i phye mas bya//mtshan rtsi lnga
14a2(rgyud): bshad zhim mos dang //'byung khungs nas byung phye ma 'am//rdo'i khaMs ni sna tshogs kyang //phye ma'i las la sbyar bar bya//de ltar rnam pa bzhi po dag//phye ma'i rgyu ra ni sgrags pa yis//lcags rnams 
14a3(rgyud): kun dang rin chen dang //'byung khungs sa dang 'bras btags pa//dkyil 'khor kun gyi las rnams la//phye ma'i don du sbyar bar bya//yang na las kyi cho ga la//phye ma dmar po sa bag bsres//nag po 
14a4(rgyud): sol ba dkar po ni//nas dang bro las byas pa gnad//bya ba rigs par 'byung ba dang //bgegs rnams tshar gcod bya ba dang //drag shul spyod pa'i las dag ni//dkyil 'khor las la 
14a5(rgyud): thal ba dang //dkyil 'khor la ni cho ga la//phye ma'i rgyu ni sngar bshad pa//kun la rung ste khyad par du//dang po laswogs rims bzhin shes//dam tshig la ni lcags kyi bzang//
14a6(rgyud): rin chen las byung dbang skul la/sa lu phye ma zhib pa la//'byung khungs las rgyas pa la//drag shul spyad pa khyad par du//thal ba laswogs bya bar shis//de'i mye ma'i cho ga'i rims//khyad par dang bcas
14a7(rgyud): stan pa yin//byang shar 'tshams nas ni//phye ma dag ni dge bar bya//g.yas su skor cing rgyun mi 'chad//thams cad mnyam zhing bar chad med//ngan pa dang ni nyams pa dang //ma snyoms pa dang bar chad 
14a8(rgyud): dang //gnod pa mang po 'byung bar 'gyur//de bas nan tan tshar byas te//mkhas pas phye ma dge bar bya//phyogs rnams kun tu mkhas pa yis//dbus su sgo ni dgod par bya ngos gnyis
14b1(rgyud): la ni cha dag kyang//bzhi bzhi dag ni dor bar bya//cha sgo chen por stegasu byas//cung zad cig ni lhag par byas//cha sgo gzhan rnams bcad pa ni//phye ma dkar po dag gis ni//sgo'i dbus kyi cha dag tu//
14b2(rgyud): ri mo 'phra mo bri bar bya//yang na sgo'i phyag rgya 'am//sgo bsrungs ji ltar 'dod pa bri//dang po mtshon cha lnga yis bri//dkyil 'khor gnyis pa rnam gsum gyis//gsum pa phye ma dkar po yis//cig rkyang du ni
14b3(rgyud): bri bar //gzhan yang kha cig gang bshad pa//de'i ri mo gcig rkyang nyid//thal phye bshad pa gang bshad pa//de dag thams cad ri mo gcig//de ltar dkyil 'khor bris nas su//cha gsum 
14b4(rgyud): dag ni legs brtags la//shin tu legs pa nges par bya//rnam par bye ba 'di dag ni//rigs gsum dag ni spyir bstan to//yang na so so'i rgyud rnams las//go rims
14b5(rgyud): ji skad bshad bzhin bri//lha'i gnas kyis bzhi rnams//ba'i lji bas sgra bar bya//de yi 'og tu khyad par du//ba'i rnam lnga'i chu yis chu//thams cad spos kyi chur gtor nas//
14b6(rgyud): de'i 'og tu phye ma dge//rig sngags rgyal po mngon sngags pa'i//spos kyi chu yis gtor bar bya//dkyil 'khor dag gi cho ga ni//rnam pa gsum po ma lus lha//lha rnam pa thams cad rgol
14b7(rgyud): ba ni//rnam pa gsum po rab tu bshad//dang po gzugs su bri bar bya//gnyis pa dag ni phyag rgya bri//gsum pa stan kyang gnas bzhin du//bri ba'i cho ga yongasu grags//blo ldan bla
14b8(rgyud): mas gzugs kyi tshul//bri ba de la mkhas par bya//rtsad mas rims te rang gi ni//ngo bo ji ltar dzaM bzhin bri//gzugs dang 'tshems dbar yan lag bdabs//gsal zhing rnam par dbye ba'o//
15a1(rgyud): @#//gzugs kyi dkyil 'khor rnams la ni//tshad dang sbyar zhing bya bar sgrags//rang gi lus la kha dog gzugs//'byung ba bzhin du gzugs bris te//ci nas yan lag mi 
15a2(rgyud): mnyam zhing //rang bzhin zhi ba'i gzugs su bri//gsang sngags cho ga shes pa yis//cho ga 'dis ni dkyil 'khor bri//blo dang ldan pas ma bslabs pas//phyag rgya rnams su dgod//
15a3(rgyud): par bya//bslab pa kyang ni phye ma yis//yan lag rdzogs par rab tu dga'//yan lag rdzogs shing rgyas pa yi//cho ga'i las ni ring du thigs//gzugs rnams legs 
15a4(rgyud): par sgrubs nas ni//mang po rnams ni zin mi 'gyur//yan lag nyams shing byin mi rlob//dngos mi 'grub par rab tu bshad//de lta bas na mkhas pa yis//
15a5(rgyud): phyag rgya'i gzugs su lha rnams dgod//yang na rigs gsum bdag po rnams//ci 'dod par ni gzugs su bkod//de la sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das//phyag rgya gtsug to rog rims bzhin/
15a6(rgyud): yid la de'i sngags zlos shing //phye ma dkar pos bri bar bya//'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang gi//phyag na pad ma rab tu sgrags//phyag na rdo rje phyag rgya ni//rdo rje rnon po rtse gsum 'bar//
15a7(rgyud): lha rnams gzhan gyi phyag rgya ni//lung las ji skad pa bshad pa bzhin//yang na rigs kyi bdag po rgya//de dang de'i spyi'i yin//lha rnams gang dang gang gi//mtshan ma gang dag gang yin pa 
15a8(rgyud): de dang de dag de'i rgya 'dir ni ma nor 'dor bar bshad//drag po'i phyag rgya rigs gsum po//bka' zlog phyag rgya rtse gsum gdeng //khyab 'jug gi ni 'khor lo rno//sde chen gyi ni 
15b1(rgyud): mdung thung yin//tshangs pa'i pad ma yin par bshad//rgya byin gyi ni rdo rjer gsungs//me'i lha'i phyag rgya bzhugs thabs yin//gshin rje'i phyag rgya be tson yin//bden bral gyi ni ral gri yin//
15b2(rgyud): chu bdag gi ni zhags par bshad//rlung gi phyag rgya rgyal mtshan yin//ku be ra'i dbyug to ste//dbang ldan gyi ni rtse gsum gsungs//'di brgyad phyogs kyang lha brgyad kyi'o//sa'i (phyi)rgya bum pa yin nya
15b3(rgyud): nyi zla'i phyag rgya zlum por bya//lha rnams so so'i bye brag las//sa si ka la sogs pa'i rgyu//mkhas pas ji skad bshad pa'i bri//yang na kun gyi zlum por bri//gang du lha rnams gzugs dag tu;
15b4(rgyud): rgod pa bshad pa der yang ni//phyag rgya dag gam yang na ni//stan tu god par rnam par bshad//de la spogs pa'i cho ga rnams//ji skad pa yi cho ga bzhin//dkyil 'khor dag tu lha smos
15b5(rgyud): kyang//cho ga bzhin du bri bar bya//de ltar cho ga dgod pa 'dis//dgod pa gnyis pa bshad pa yin//stan tu dgod pa'i cho ga ni//'di las gzhan pa gsum par bshad//dkyil
15b6(rgyud): 'khor dag gi nang du ni//mtshon rtsi phya ma thig le bri//dkyil 'khor gyi ni tshad las ni//kho ra khor yug rgya cher bskor//rigs kyi bdag po'i lha rnams dang //go rims bzhin du de ltar bya//
15b7(rgyud): lha rnams gzhan gyi gdan rnams ni//spyi'i cho ga bshad pa ni//zlum po 'am yang na gru bzhir ru//thog mar gdan ni dgod par bya//yid la de yi sngags bzlas shing//de'i steng du mkhas
15b8(rgyud): pas dgod//phyi rol du ni thig le la//mkhas pas lha rnams thams cad dgod//thams cad thig ler gnas byas nas//spyan drangs par ni rtsam par bya//'di dag sdod du dgod bya ste//
16a1(rgyud): @#//gsum par yongasu grags pa yin//rings sam yang na mi nus na//stan tu brjod pa'i dkyil 'khor bya//yang na cho ga shes pa yis gsum po de'i cho ga ni//re re 'am
16a2(rgyud): gnyis sam sum char ram//sres nas dkyil 'khor legs par bri//rigs kyi bdag po gsum rnams ni//gsang sngags thog mar bkod nasu//dkyil 'khor nang du mkhas pa yis
16a3(rgyud): lhag ma rnams ni phyag rgya bri//phyi rol du ni stan rnaMs bri//'di ni spyi'i cho ga ste//dkyil 'khor dag la khyad par du//bar ma dag la sngags pa yin//gzugs rnams legs par ma rdzogs na//
16a4(rgyud): nyes ma sngar ni bshad pa bzhin//phyag rgya bas na//gnas rnams stongs par 'gyur nas ni//cho ga tha ma mi shiso//phyag rgya rnamasu gang bkod pa//bar ma dag tu grags pa yi//
16a5(rgyud): khyad par du ni de sngags yin//de la byin gyis rlob par 'gyur//phyag rgya bris nas bde ba ste//dbus kyi sta phyag rgya gang yin de nyid lha//de bas mkhas pas nan tan du dkyil 'khor
16a6(rgyud): du ni phyag bri//dkyil 'khor du ni rnamasu//ma bshad kyang ni dgos med par//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'og logasu//gzhan gyis mi thub lha mo bri//dbus kyi stong pa gang yin pa//
16a7(rgyud): der yang bum pa'i steng du ni//shes rab pha rol phyin pa gzhag //dkyil 'khor ma lus de ni mchod cing blag par bya//la la gang yang ma gsungs kyang//'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang
16a8(rgyud): gis/drung du blo dang ldan pa yis//gos dgar can ni rtag tu 'bri//phyag na rdo rje'i drung du ni//bdud rtsi thabs sbyor kun tu bri//brtson 'grus chen po ma ma ki//phyag rgya chen po bri bar bya/
16b1(rgyud): cho ga shes pa sgo drung du//dga'o dang ni nye dga' bri//mkhas pas ji ltar bshad pa yi//'khrog ma de bzhin mdun du bri//sgo rnams kun tu rdo rje bri//phyogs skyong phyag rgya'ang lugs bzhin
16b2(rgyud): du //so sor drag po'i gzugasu ni//so so'i phyogs kyi gnasu bri//gnod sbyin nor bu bzang laswogs//stan pa la ni bdag dga' ba//sgo'i byang gi phyi rol du//phyi rol du ni de bkod//
16b3(rgyud): gang dag tu ni ma bshad kyang//shar du sangs rgyas thams cad kyang//de bzhin chos dang dge 'dun la//gus pas dkyil 'khor byas pas mchod//'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa dang//
16b4(rgyud): rgyal sras mthu chen thob pa dang//namakha'i snying po don grub dang//dri ma med pa'i blo gros dang//de laswogs pa'i sems dpa' che//dkyil 'khor gyi ni phyi rol dgod//
16b5(rgyud): bskal pa bzang po'i grogs pa ni//byaMs pa laswogs thaMd bkod//rdo rje'i sde dang lag bzangs dang//de bzhin bgegs kyi sde dpon dang dbang phyug chen po chung
16b6(rgyud): mar bcas//tshangs pa mes po chen po bri//rigasngags 'chang gi rgyal po yang//glang po che zhes bya ba dang//yan lag bkram 'byin rtse dran can//g.yon phyogs spyod pa ni mag bzangs dang//
16b7(rgyud): sna tshogs sde dang legs par 'grub//rigasngags 'chang ni bdag po ste//bdun po 'di dag rigs kyi rtso//dkyil 'khor dag ni thams cad kyi//dngos grub pa ste/mthu can rnams//lho'i phyogasu
16b8(rgyud): legs par bkod//ma mo rnams dang sna g.yon //de bzhin bza' rnams sna tshogs dang//sgra gcan stobs can rab gsil dang//rnaM par snang byed laswogs dang nor rgyas laswogs klu rnaMs dang 
17a1(rgyud): @#//de dag nub kyi phyogasu bkod//shar du rdo rje'i phyag rgya ni//rgya byin lha dang bcas pa dang//de bzhin gnas rtsang lha rnams dang//gus par yang ni mchod par mdzad//nyi ma gnyis kyi 
17a2(rgyud): dkyil 'khor ni//kar dang dmar ba'i bye brag gis//shar dang nub tu bris nas ni//de nas de gnyis mkhas pas mchod//me'i phyogasu bzhugs thabs rgya//me lha drang srong 'khor bcas bri//lho 
17a3(rgyud): phyogs be tson phyag rgya ni//gshin rje yi dgas sogs bcas//bden bral phyogasu ral gri rgyal//bden bral ba'i srin por bcas//chu lha phyogasu zhags pa rgya//chu lha klu dang lhan cig tu//
17a4(rgyud): rlung gi phyogasu rgyal mtshan rgya//byang du dbyug to'i phyag rgya yang//ku be ra ni gnod sbyin bcas//dbang ldan phyogasu rtse gsuM rgya//dbang ldan dang ni lhan cig tu//'og tu bum pa'i 
17a5(rgyud): phyag rgya yi//sa'i lha mo lha min bcas//phyogs skyong rnams kyang go rims bzhin//de ltar gnas su dgod byas nas bdug spos me tog laswogs pas//mkhas pas so sor 
17a6(rgyud): 'khor bcas mchod//mdzod spu dang ni mdung thung dang//'khor lo sgrub la cher 'byung dang //rigasngags chen mo spyan dang ni//yid bzhin nor bu rgyal mtshan dang//mngag gzhug 
17a7(rgyud): brtson 'grus chen po dang//de bzhin gzhan gyis mi thub dang//de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs bdag gis//dkyil 'khor laswogs dgod par bya//drag shul mo dang skad chen po//
17a8(rgyud): de bzhin gas dkar can dang ni//rtan dang ni ral pa gcig//sgrol ma dkar sham dpal chen mo 'dod pa sbyor byed gsang sngags dang//'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang gi//
17b1(rgyud): dkyil 'khor ma lus pa rnamasu//mkhas pas cho ga bzhin tu bkod//rdo rje lcags kyu khu tshur dang//rig pa'i rgyal po phyag rgyar bcas//khro bo chen po thal sbyar dang//gom pa 'dor ba nyid 
17b2(rgyud): dang ni//rdo rje lu gu rgyud chen dang//khro bo 'khol ba zhes bya dag//phyag na rdo rje'i dkyil 'khor du//ma smos kyang ni de dag gi//dkyil 'khor dang po nang du ni//ji skad smos pa'i lha bkod nas
17b3(rgyud): de ni mkhas pas dgyil 'khor ni//gnyis pa rim pa ji bzhin du//mo smos pa dang 'dod pa yi//gsang sngags rnams ni bkod bya zhing//'khrul pa med par gsum pa yang//bye brag tu ni dgod par
17b4(rgyud): byaIrgyud kun gyi ni sngags dag dang//yongasu mnyes par bya ba'i phyir//mkhas pas dkyil 'khor phyi rol du//spyi'i cho ga gzhan 'di bya//byang dang lho dang shar phyogs kyi//
17b5(rgyud): phyogs gsum du ni stegs bu bya//byas pa'i rim pa gsum bsags na//spyan drang ba yi cho ga bya//de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs rnams ni//gsang sngags kun la dbulo 
17b6(rgyud): zhes//metog spos chus bkang ba yi//mchod yon dag ni dbul bar bya//sangs rgyas pa la shar phyogasu//gsang ba'i bdag pos bshad pa yi//gsang sngags rna sa ni lho phyogasu//
17b7(rgyud): de ltar byas nas rigs kun gyi//gsang sngags dang 'khor dang bcas pa kun//mchod pa byas pa 'di ru ni//degs par 'gyur bar gnon mi za//cho ga 'di yis nub phyogasu//'jig
17b8(rgyud): rten pa yi lha rnams ni//kun la dbulo zhes brjod do//phyi rol stegs bu'i steng du mchod//zhi ba la ni cho ga la//byang chub sems dpa' dkon mchog gsum//rtsa nga ma'i gnas na
18a1(rgyud): @#//'khod pa yi//lha rnams kyang ni dgod par bya//rgyas pa la ni khyad par du//rig sngags gsang sngags thams cad dag//bstan pa dag la dga' ba yi//gnod 
18a2(rgyud): sbyin rnams kyang dgod par bya//drag shul spyod pa'i dkyil 'khor du//khro bo rnams kyi phyag rgya ni//drag shul chen po rnams dag dang//dpag gzhug pho mo mang po bkod//dkyil 
18a3(rgyud): 'khor dag ni thams cad la//las rnams gsum du shes par bya//de bas cho ga 'di rnams kyi//dkyil 'khor rnam pa gsum du bshad//dkyil 'khor dag ni dbus dag na//
18a4(rgyud): sa stong yongs su ma rtogs pa//gang na yod pa de ru ni//shes rab pha rol phyin pa gzhag//yang na sgo'i g.yos rol na//slob ma bzang po'i steng du gzhag//g.yon 
18a5(rgyud): gyi phyogs na sbyin sreg phyir//me ni yam sreg shing gis sbar//yang na shar lho 'tshams dag tu//me ni rab tu sbar bar bya//yang na las kyi rjes 'thun pa'i 
18a6(rgyud): phyogs kyi go rims bzhin du gzhag//dri rtsang khang ngam gnas khang ngam//phug gam yang ni sgo skabs sam//dog sa zhig tu byed na ni//der ni ji ltar 'dod par bya//go skabs 
18a7(rgyud): dog pa dag tu ni//sgrub pa'i dkyil 'khor ma rtogs pa//slob dpon gyis ni gzhan mi 'dri//dris na mod la 'chi bar 'chi 'gyur//dkyil 'khor dag ni ma lus par//
18a8(rgyud): go skabs yangs pa'i phyogs su shis//khang bzangs dag gam 'khyams su yang//gom pa yis ni ci bder bri//rang rong dag dang sdong dum dag//ljon shing dag dang rdo ba ni
18b1(rgyud): rab tu sgrims te dbyung byas te//de dag zug rngu yin par bshad//mkhas pas de dbyung thabs med na //zhi ba'i cho ga byas te gzhag//snying po'-i dkyil 'khor du ni de//bag med
18b2(rgyud): nam yang de mi gzhag//rang rong laswogs bye brag dag//dkyil 'khor dbus su ma rtogs par//gnas khang laswogs nyes med te//de dag de nas rang bzhin gnas//
18b3(rgyud): khang bzangs dag laswogs par ni/gsang rtor byas la dag par bshad/ri las sa bzhi mnyam pa dang/rdo yi zug rngu rtag yi mi dgos//gal te gnas kyi dbang gis su/lha rnams
18b4(rgyud): phyogs log dgod byed na//mkhas pas zhi ba byas te bya//snying po nam yang mi bya'o//bya ba'-i dbang gis de laswogs//'gal ba gang dang gang byed pa //de dang
18b5(rgyud): der ni mi mkhas pas//thams cad zhi ba'i las kyis gsal//blo dang ldan pas cho ga 'di//dkyil 'khor rdzogs par bris nas ni//yid kyis legs par mnyam
18b6(rgyud): gzhag nas//de'i 'og tu ma rtags te//rgya la 'ba' zhig ma legs na//de ni phyir yang bcos par 'gyur//mkhas pas rang rang gsang sngags sam//slob ma'-i sngags kyang
18b7(rgyud): 'dod na bkod //slob ma rtag pa la la zhig//gal te dbang gi bskur 'dod na//dbang skur don du dkyil 'khor ni//phye ma dkar pos bri bar bya//dkyil 'khor dag ni rjes 'thun
18b8(rgyud): par//stegs bu gru bzhi byas nas su//de'i dbus su tshon sna lngas//pad ma yongasu rdzogs par bris//bkra shis la swogs mtshan ma rnams//de ni ji ltar 'dod pa 'dri//bkra
19a1(rgyud): @#//shis phyir ni gang stan pa //yang na de'i dngos grub dgod//mkhas pas g.yos rnams gang gzhag pa'i//ras ris rnams kyang phye mas rtsang//g.yo su byas pa'i phye ma 
19a2(rgyud): kun//bsogs nas der ni dgod par bya//bum pa dag dang rgyal mtshan dang//rta babs gzhag pa'i si ris dang//phyi rol mdzes par bya ba dang//ji ltar 'dod pa phye mas byas//
19a3(rgyud): de nas gzhan yang lha rnams ni//spyan drang ba'i cho ga bya//mchod pa'i go r-ims ji bzh-in du//yang dag mdor bsdus bshad par bya//dkyil 'khor cho ga bzh-in bris nas//nye reg bya zhing 
19a4(rgyud): rtsang mar bya lha rnams la yang phyag byas nas//bzang ngam mi bzang rtag par bya//phyi rol shar phyogs kha bltas nas//yid kyis dkyil 'khor bdag po yi//snying pos gasnga
19a5(rgyud): sngags kun zlas shing//mtshan ma dag ni rtag par bya//ji srid bltas ni bzang po dag//'gal zhing 'byung bar 'gyur gyi bar//de srid sgrims te yid cig tu//
19a6(rgyud): zlas brjod byas pa 'bad cing sdad//de mthong blo ldan rangs gyur pas//las kun byed pa'i gsang sngags pas//bdag g+sis gang zhig bzlas pa yis//srung ba legs par spyad par bya//
19a7(rgyud): yang na rgyud rnams kun gyi phyir//bgyegs rnams thams cad bzlog pa la//rdo rje be con brtson 'grus che//khro bo bdud rtsa thal sbyor dang//mgo la rdo rje smos pa yis//phur bu ra 
19a8(rgyud): bo dra ba gsum//blo dang ldan pas lnga pa dag//cho ga bzhin du sbyar bar bya//bsrungs ma rtsa ba'i gsang sngags sam//yang na de y-i snying po byaHbla ma la yang y-id kyis su//srungs ma
19b1(rgyud): snga ma bzhin du bya//snga ma byas nas de 'og tu//mkhas pas dkyil 'khor bzhug par bya//zhugs nas mchod yon snod thogs te//mchod yon dag kyang dbul bar bya//mchod yon 
19b2(rgyud): snod ni gser dngul lam//zangs sam shel lam nor bu 'am//'ching bu dkar po las byas pa//shing ngam de bzhin rdo las byas//dung ngam 'o ma skyogs pa 'am//nya phy-is dgag ma 
19b3(rgyud): kham phor ni//gsar pa 'am yan lag nyams pa//dag par byas la blang bar bya//'dir ni khro rgyas zlas pa yis//me tog dag kyang bcug nasu//mnyam par gzhag ste dkyil 'khor gyi//
19b4(rgyud): bsang sngags lan bdun zlas nas ni//snga ma bzhin du dri chu yis//mchod yon gus pas dbul bar bya//dkyil 'khor dbu mar phul nas ni//lhag ma rnams la gtor bar 
19b5(rgyud): bshad//dkyil 'khor thams cad gsang rtar byas//de nas cho ga 'di byas so//bum pa nag po rir med//gzugs bzang kha dog rab tu dmar//gsar pa mra chag ma gas
19b6(rgyud): pa yang na dbyibs ni legs shing snum//spos chu dag gis bkang ba la//'bru rnams kun dang ldan byas shing//sman rnams dang yang ldan pa dang//rin chen kun dang rab tu ldan//
19b7(rgyud): mgu la pa ras ni gsar pas bcings//me tog phreng bas rnam par brgyan//kha brgyan la bu bzang pos byas//shing tog bkra shis par blugs pa//'bras bu me tog sil mar ldan//
19b8(rgyud): spos kyis byug cing bdug pas bdug//thams cad bkra shis dang ldan pas//bzlas brjod lan bdun byas pa dag//bkra shis don du gnyer ba yis//phyogs dang phyogs 'tshams dag tu
20a1(rgyud): @#//dgod//yon tan kun dang ldan pa yi//bum pa 'di kun ma 'byor na//dbang skur phyir ni 'bring po gcig//nan tan bskyed te legs par gzhag (ga) dang sgo n-i ma lus par
20a2(rgyud): bum pa dag ni gzhag nasu //bar sgo chen po gnyisu shis//khyad par du ni gzhag par bya//bum pa gcig ni phyi sgo yi//phyogasu mngon par bzhag par bya//mang po 'byor par ma
20a3(rgyud): gyur na//brgyad dam yang na bzhi gzhag go//nyung du rnams ni khyad par du//phyi rol dag tu dgod byas te//sgo'-i drung du gcig la gzhag//de ni nges par bya dgos so//'bras kyi so dang
20a4(rgyud): mon sran dang //nas dang bro dang til dang lnga//'bru rnams kun shes bya ba yin//las rnams kun ni 'di dag bzung ;sing hi bya kri kar ni ka//ha sa ha sa de ba ste//sman rnaMs
20a5(rgyud): kun shes bya ba la //lnga po 'di dag shes par bya//byuru gser gyi sa le sgam (   )dung dang mu tig nor bu ste//rin chen kun shes bya ba la (   )lnga po 'di dag shes par (   )bya//rgyal mtshan gsang ba
20a6(rgyud): sna tshogs dag//brgyad ni kho ra khor yug tu//yu bi rang po drang po la//spyangs nas nye bar rab gzigs te//dkar po shar gyi phyogasu gzugs//shar lho 'tshamasu le ba rgan gzugs//lho nub 'tshaMsu
20a7(rgyud): dung dkar gzugs ;nub kyi phyogasu dmar po gzugs//nub byang 'tshamasu khra bo gzugs//byang gi 'tshamasu ser po gzugs//byang shar 'tshamasu ljang ser gzugs//de ltar rgyal mtshan brgyad po 
20a8(rgyud): rnams;go rims bzhin du gzugs par bya//rgyal mtshan rnaMs ky-i rtse mo la//tog gi rma bya'i mdongs kyis brgyan//rgyal mtshan ma rnaMs ni ma rnyed na//rgyal mtshan thal dkar gcig bu 'aM//yang na de bas
20b1(rgyud): mang du yang rung//shar gyi phyogasu gzugs par bya//pog por kham pa lasogs pa'i//so ma btang bar kham por bcug//byas nas phyogs dang phyogs 'tshams ni//rgyab dang de bzhin na dug
20b2(rgyud): du bkod//pog por dgi ni ma 'byor na//kham pa'i phar pa'i nang nas dbul//de dag kyang ni ma 'byor na//cig cig sgor ni gzhag ste dbul//me tog phreng bas brgyan pa yi//rta babs dag kyang
20b3(rgyud): phyogs bzhir bya//nas dang bro dang 'bru rnams kyi//myu gu legs par bskyed par dgod//dril bu rdugs dang rnga yab dang//srad bu dag kyang phyogs bzhir ni//rta babs la swogs phyi rol du//mdzes par
20b4(rgyud): bya phyir mkhas pas btags//'bras dang 'bru dang bcas pa yi//me tog sil ma rab tu bkram//dkyil 'khor dgi gi phyi rol du//phye ma'i ri mo sdug gu bri//'jig rten 'di na
20b5(rgyud): grags pa yi//bkra shis la swogs gang yin rnams//dkyil 'khor gyi ni phyi rol du//phyogs bzhi dag tu mkhas pas rgod//de ltar rgyas dang mdor bsdus pas ci nus pa
20b6(rgyud): yi sbyor ba bya//cho ga bzhin du byas nasu//de nas spyan drang rtsam par bya//mchod yon cho ga snga ma bzhin//ma yengs par ni sbyar nasu//so so sngags kyis ci rigs par//mkhas pas spyan ni drang bar
20b7(rgyud): bya//dkyil 'khor bdag po'i gsang sngags sam//yang na thams cad snying pos bya//sngags brgyud las ni ci bshad pa//de bzhin du ni spyan drang ba//mkhas pas de bzhin gshegs rigs la//'khro
20b8(rgyud): lo bsgyur bas spyan drang ngo//yang na spyan drang cho ga ni//sangs rgyas spyan gyis byar yang rung//phyag na pad ma'i rigs kun la//rig pa'i rgyal po spyan drang bya//yang na spyan drang cho gni//dpal mo las kun
21a1(rgyud): @#//byed pas bya mkhas pas rdo rje 'dzin rigs la//gnod mdzes zhes bya ba'i gsang sngags sam//ma yengs par ni ma ma ki//spyan drang ba yi cho ga bya//yang na spyan drang cho ga ni//
21a2(rgyud): sngags rgyud bdag pos sngags gang yin//de 'am de yi snying po 'di//lha rnams kyis ni ming smos nas//mkhas pas spyan drang cho ga bya//yang na rang gi rigs kyi sngags//gang yin de 'am de 
21a3(rgyud): snying ngam cho ga ji skad gsungs pas kyang //spyan drang ba ni mkhas pas bya//yang na phyi ma nang dag gi//rang gi gsang sngags gang yin pa//de dag gis ni spyan drang ngam//ci ltar lob par byar yang 
21a4(rgyud): rung //mchod yon gyi ni cho ga bzhin//de ltar spyan drangs byas nas ni//mchod yon nyid kyis lan re zhig//de nas zhabs bkru'i cho ga bya bder gshegs pa zhes byas swogs pa//phyag
21a5(rgyud): kyang gus pas 'tshal ba dag//go rims bzhin du byas nas ni//mkhas pas de dag mchod par brtsam//dang po byug spos dbul byas te//de'i 'og tu me tog dbul//de nas legs 
21a6(rgyud): par mnyam gzhag nas//bdug spos lha bshos rnams kyang dbul//tsan dan a kar gur gum dang //ud pal rgya spos kri yang gu//lo shing shing rtsa spang spos mig//na ga pus spa na ga pu//
21a7(rgyud): dran ti su ga smel ba ta dang //na laM sti'u na ra sa ra ldan//rdo grag sha dang pus pa dang //sna ma'i lam tsha rol dang //ka ti ma tu ri ka dang //d+h+ya n+ya ka po la swogs bzang //'bras bu thang chu'i tshogs rnams ni//mkhas 
21a8(rgyud): pa rnams kyis 'di spyar te//mkhas pas blo dang ldan pa yis//phyi dang nang gi lha kun la//go rims bzhin tu gsang sngags kyis//mngon par sngags nas byug spos dbul//srog chags yan 
21b1(rgyud): lag sbyar mi rung//rgya skyegs chang spags sbyar mi rung//rdzas ngan dag kyang sbyar mi rung//rtsa ba mo sbyar ba'ang mi rung ngo//sangs rgyas li na khyad par du//byug spos gur gum las byas pa
21b2(rgyud): 'am//a kar nag po'i dri zhim pa//ga phus kyis sgos dbul bar bya//'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang gis//tsan dan dkar po rab tu dbul//phyag na rdo rje 'khor bcas la//tsan dan dmar
21b3(rgyud): po dbul bar bya//lha mo rnams la byug spos dag//dza ltar rigs par spyir phul nas//me tog skam las skyes pa dag//chu las skyes pas mchod par bya//ba li ba kun kun rta dang//ma ma
21b4(rgyud): ti na ma li ka//tsam pa ko sho bu na ga//pri yang ku ti ur yan//ga sar sa la ti ka la//shing gi ud pal ka ti ni//ka ta ra dang bi ra dang//a dzo dog pha ka dam dang//pi na dar
21b5(rgyud): dang ka ta ra//ka tsa na dang pa ta la//shi la ta gan ti gur ra pa//pa na ta na gi shu nyid//cho ga shes pas de la sogs//skam las skyes pa go rims bzhin//nan tan du ni
21b6(rgyud): dbul bya zhing //ngan pa dag ni rnam par spangs//gan ta ku ya shig mu dang//pa sha ya pa ti mu ta dang//ma grol tra ka ti ba dang//ke ta ka dang ma na yan//ku na ta dang mang ga dang//ku ri pa
21b7(rgyud): la ma li ka//de la swogs pa mi shes pa//drag shul spyod pa sbyar bar bya//pad ma dkar dang pad ma dmar//ud pal rigs ni sna tshogs dang//ku mu ta la chu skyes rnams//dkyil 'khor las la
21b8(rgyud): sbyar byas te//de yang ku mu ta dmar dang//de bzhin si'u gan ti kar//dri mi zhim pa gzhan dag kyang//drag shul spyod pa sbyar ba ra bya//ge sar tra ni ka ra dang//ma ma ti dang me tog dkar dang//dri zhim
22a1(rgyud): @#//dag ni khyad par du//de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs la dbul//pad ma la swogs chu skyes rnams//mkhas pas rigs la dbul//a ga sho u ka ti la ka//
22a2(rgyud): de bzhin na ba na li ka//kri yaM ku dang ti pa ra//ka ra bi ra trag tra ga|ud pa la sngon po ny-id dag kyang //rdo rje'i rigs la dbul bar bya//de bzhin gshegs pa'i r-igs rnams la//tsan dan a 
22a3(rgyud): kar bdug spos dbul//mkhas pas sri re be sta sogs//thang chu pad ma'i rigs la dbul//a dkar nag po gu gul gyi//bdug spos rdo rje y-i rigaso//bdug spos dag ni spyir bshad na//srog chag+sa
22a4(rgyud): yan lag spangs pa'i rdzas//tsan dan ga pur a kar dang //du ru ka dang spos kar dang //sri par sta dang sra rtsi bog//da ras gu gul shal phyi ba kyi//u shi li ding ba la ka//spang spos
22a5(rgyud): bla sgang ga pur dang //de ba tha gru thag shing dang //de la sogs pa'i rdzas rnams ni//ji snyed pa dang bsdu byas nas//blo dang ldan bas bu ram dang //lhan cig tu ni sbyar bar bya//de lta bu'i
22a6(rgyud): bdug spos na//lha la go rims bzhin du dbul//de dag gang yang rung ba dang //gzhan yang dri zhim bshad pa gang //rdzas mang dag dang sbyar ba yaM bdug spos dkyil 'khor dag la dbul//dkyil 'khor
22a7(rgyud): gyi ni tshig bzhin rab dang sems kyis ci 'byor pa//bdug spos me tog la sogs pas//bya ba dag ni legs par bya//me tog de dag rgyun mkhan gyis//rgyus pa zhig na ngan pa yis//nyung shas yod na
22a8(rgyud): mi 'gal te//de mang na ni rnam par spang //mi shes pa yi byug spos dag//mkhas bas dkyil 'khor dbul mi bya//srog chags yan lag gis bdug pa//de yis gzhan dag bdug mi bya//rgya skyegs
22b1(rgyud): la swogs bdug spos ni//dge ba'i las la dbul mi bya//de 'dra'i cho ga 'dir shes nas//cho ga'i las la 'bad par bya//phyi rol gzhag pa'i pog por dang //kham por rab tu bsgrags pa gang //de dag tu ni 
22b2(rgyud): gang 'dod par//srog chags yan lag sbyar ba gzhag//dkyil 'khor phyi rol 'jig rten pa'i//lha ni gang dag dri med pa'i//de la mkhas pas de bzhin du//bdug spos byug spos ci dga' dbul//
22b3(rgyud): rigs kyi bdag po rnams la ni//mchod pa khyad par can du bya//de'i 'og tu lha kun la//gral rims bzhin du mchod par bya//rigs kyi bdag po rnams las ni//bdug spos snod ni rnam gsum gzhag//
22b4(rgyud): dbus su bdug spos snod cig ni//bdug spos 'dul ba rgyun mi 'chad//dkyil 'khor rim pa re re la//bdug spos snod ni re re gzhag//de nyid nas ni thams cad du//rtag par bdug 
22b5(rgyud): spos dbul bar bya//yang na thams cad spyi la ni//bdug spos snod na gcig gzhag ste//bsang rtor yang dang yang byas nas//de nas bdug spos dbul bar bya//de'i 'og tu dkyil 'khor du//
22b6(rgyud): lha rnams kun la mchod yon phul//yang na yid la bsngags bzlas la//lha bshos dbul ba nyid du bya//lha bshos dbul ba'i 'byor pa dang //rtsang zhing cha yang mnyam par ru//dkyil 'khor 
22b7(rgyud): gyi ni go rims bzhin//mkhas pas nan tan snyed de bya//rigs kyi bdag po dang rnams la ni//dbul ba khyad par can du bya//dkyil 'khor gyi ni bdag po la//lha bshos dag ni nyis 'gyur 
22b8(rgyud): phul//'og ma rnams la go rims bzhin//mkhas bas de la chud du dbul//lha bshos dbus pa bar bar du//ci dgar khyad par can gyis bya//mchod pa'i bshos ni bzang po dang 
23a1(rgyud): @#//rtsang zhing rab tu rgya chen zhing //dkyil 'khor dag tu lha rnams kun//rab tu mnyes shing dga' bar 'gyur//dbul bar byas ba rnams (ba)ni//bar du kha cig chad gyur na//de las gzhan pa dag 
23a2(rgyud): pa dag la ni//mkhas pas lha bshos lhag de dbul//dbul ba ha cang nyums pa rnams//rigs kyi bdag pos dbul bar bya//yang na snying po'i dkyil 'khor du//rigs kyi spyi la ma lus kun//yang na 
23a3(rgyud): gang dang gang bshams pas//ded mkhas pas thams cad du//dkyil 'khor dang po 'am gnyis pa 'am//gsum po dag tu dbul byas nas//rigs bdag rnams dang khyad med pa'i//nyes pa 'ga' yang yod
23a4(rgyud): min pas//dkyil 'khor ni mi rtsam pas//bag dang chas te rtag par bya//dang po chu shing pad mo swags//lo ma 'dab ma bting byas nas//swasti ka la sogs pa yi//bca' ba dag ni
23a5(rgyud): dbul bya zhing //de bzhin 'bras chan la sogs pa yi//bza' ba dag kyang rims bzhin dbul//de'i 'og tu rdo khug dang //bca' ba 'bras bu la sogs dbul//gtor ma yi ni las
23a6(rgyud): dag la//bca' ba rnam pa sna tshogs pas//nas dang bro las byas pa yi//dri zhim pa dang legs pa'ang dbul//hags ni rnam pa sna tshogs dang //la ba du dag dang lab ni//se nam du shi shi dang //
23a7(rgyud): snum gur a sho bar ta dang //shra ta ka tshi la ka dang //par pa ha dang par ma ni//ra tsa ha sa 'gu he dang //kar po da ka ri ka nyi dang //pu sha par ta ha bi dang //pur rna de bzhin the'u dang //ba ti pus pus bai ta
23a8(rgyud): ma|b+hin d+ha kha dang kha rdzu kha//sa rdza kha dang man da kha//dza li ti bi kha dang ni//shas ku dang mo da dang //snum phor sna tshogs kha rar bcas//u lo bi ka dang ku be//de bzhin du ni blang rna dang //
23b1(rgyud): ma shing khur ba laswogs pas//sha sa ka dang lo'-i dbyings//ba rta ka dang be reg dang//swo ya li ka dra tshe dang//de laswogs te sna tshogs pa'i//bca' ba dag ni 'dir dbulo//sri sa ra dang 'o thug dang//
23b2(rgyud): 'bras thug chu dang sbyar ba dang//zho dang dar ba nas thug dang//kha dog dmar dang ser swogs sgyur//lo ma bting ba de dag gi//kham por dag nas dbul bar bya//de nas sa lu 'am drug
23b3(rgyud): bcu yi//'bras chan bu sa me dbul//de nas tshod ma sna tshogs pa//g.yos legs pa la dro zhim pa dang//sran chung tshod ma lasogs pa'i//tshod ma rnam pa sna tshogs dbul//de nas sa
23b4(rgyud): 'bras chan zho sbyar bu ram mar//kham por dag nas dbu la bar bya//mkhas pas zas rnams bzang ba dag//gang yang rung ba'i lha bshos rnams//cho ga shes pa'i zas rnaMs
23b5(rgyud): ni//dkar gsum 'byung po thaMd la//rgya chen dag ni sna tshogs pa'i//ji ltar rigs par sbyar bar bya//'bras chen mar zho (ma)mor sbyar//dkar gsum zhes bya 'o dug dang//dri sar sran tshod
23b6(rgyud): dang sbyar ba//de ming 'byung po thams cad pa'o//bca' ba mang bcas rgya chen te;nas thug dang sna tshogs pa'o//de ltar kha zas rnam pa drug/tsa nu+is par ni dbul bar bya//dri mi zhim dang ska ba dang;kha
23b7(rgyud): ba dang ni tsha ba dang//tshod ma zhag tu lon pa dang//bkra mi shis pa rnam par spang//tshod ma sna tshogs ma 'byor na//sran tshod cig tu dbul bar bya//zho ni kun du 'bras chan gyi//steng du skos te blug+sa
23b8(rgyud): par bya//'o thug ma sha'i sran tshod dang//zho dang 'bras chan sbyin sreg dag ni de bzhin sgrags//yang na re re'-i phyed du lnga//zhi ba'i sngags kyis so sor sbyar//gsang sngags thaMd rims bzhin du//
24a1(rgyud): @#//swa sti ka//kri pa ra dang drug po dag//gtor ma'i las la rtag mi spang //dbul ba'i rtor ma'i las la ni//drug po kho nar rdzogs par bya//de dag gang yang rung ba zhig//nyams na rtor ma'i las
24a2(rgyud): nyamaso//'o thug nad du spang rtsi btab//de bzhin zho dang bu ram btab//sran tshod nad du mar gyis btab//bdog na skye'u sher dag kyang bdab//'bras bu dag dang rtsa ba dang //rnam pa sna tshogs sgrims
24a3(rgyud): te btab//lha rnams dang ni gsang sngags dag//khyad par du yang de la dges//am brag ba sa po se'u//rgum 'brum rgya shug tog tu ka//'bras bu ka byin tsho nus ra da dang //na ra ka dang pa na pa//rgyu shing 
24a4(rgyud): la gu chu shing 'bras//b+h+ye bya pa ra ba d+ha dang //shing dza ma bu dang skyur ru ra//kar ma ta bi ra yam dang //de la sogs pa'i 'bras bu ni//rnam pa sna tshogs sman pa dag//mkhas pa'i sbyar ma rtor ma 
24a5(rgyud): las la ni//gus pa dag tu dbul bar bya//na ra ki li pi la'i bras//ta la dang ni se 'bru dang //de la swogs pa gel ba yi//'bras bu dag ni rnam par spang //rdo ba yi rdo bu dang ni//bi ta ra la swogs 
24a6(rgyud): pa yi//ldum bu bshus pa la sogs dag//thams cad du ni dbul bar bya//su ra na yi rtsa ba dang //ka du ka da'i nyid dang ni//de la sogs pa'i rdo gu ni//mi shes pa dag rnam par spang //'bras bu'i mchog ni 
24a7(rgyud): bal po se'u//rdo gu'i mchog ni bi si ri//de dag blo dang ldan pa yis//sgrims te dkyil 'khor dag tu dbul//sran chen rnam pa sna tshogs dang //til ni la chos dang sres pa//de yi dkyil 'khor phyi 
24a8(rgyud): rol gyi//lha la go rims bzhin du bya//de nas me tog dkar po dang //til dang 'bras yos dkyil 'khor 'gram//tha ma phyogs skyong rtor ma ni//rgya chen phyogs rnams kun tu sbyar//'bras yos
24b1(rgyud): dang ni til tu bcas//'bras chan me tog chu dang bcas//sran chan bcas shing mang du bya//mar dang tshag chog bcas pa yi//rtor ma 'di 'dra slob dpon gyis//lag tu blangs te bka' bzhin du//phyogs 'tshams
24b2(rgyud): kun tu gsum gsum dbul//mchod yon dang ni bdug pa sbyin//srin po rnams dang sha za dang//'byung po rnams dang gshin rje dang//sems can rnam pa sna tshogs pa//mkhas pas rtor ma phyogasu dbul//
24b3(rgyud): sems la gang dang grang dran pa//de dag kun la gtor ma sbyin//sa phyogs de yi sems can ni//ljon shing ge ser bcas rnams la//sbyin bya gal te gtor ma che//blo bur lce sbyang sngags na ni//de na mnyam
24b4(rgyud): par ma 'khrul par//gtor ma yang dang yang du sbyin//lce sbyang sgra dang sgra chen dang//'byung bo dag ni snang ba dang//ljon shing yal ka chag swogs dang//'brug gi sgra ni grag pa dang
24b5(rgyud): de la swogs pa ya mtshan bltas//gal te der ni 'byung 'gyur//gtor ma ji ltar rigs sbyin nas//bsrung ba'i las kyi bya bar bya//ci thos ji ltar shes pa dang//ci de se mthong ri ba
24b6(rgyud): ji lta bar//lha rnams kun la phyogs dag tu//de bzhin gtor ma sbyin par bya//de ltar phyogs kyi gtor ma dag//byin nas legs par lag bkrus te//nye reg byas nas phyi rol du//mkhas pas
24b7(rgyud): yis ni bdug spos sbyin//de nas nang du zhugs pa dang//mchod yon snga ma bzhin phul nas//bdug spos dag gis phul nasu//yid kyis mchod pa'i bshos rnams phul//dkyil 'khor dbu ma'i
24b8(rgyud): las rnams ni//thams cad la yang nan tan du//ras ris dkar la 'phra ba dag//re re la yang zung re dbul//'byor na lha rnaMs thams cad la//ras ris zung re dbul bar bya//ma 'byor
25a1(rgyud): @#//na yang rigs gsum gyi//bdag po rnams la zung de dbul//yang na cho ga shes pa yis//dkyil 'khor dbus su ras dran ni//zung cig slob ma'i steng gzhag ste//phul nas kun la
25a2(rgyud): sngo bar bya//de nas so so'i gsang sngags ni//re re la yang bdun bdun bzlas//dkyil 'khor bdag po'i gsang sngags kyang //brgya rtsa brgyad du bzlas pa bya//yang na rigs kyi snying po rnams//re re zhing yang
25a3(rgyud): brgya rtsa bzlas//de nas so so'i phyag rgya rnams//bzlas brjod gsum byas rims bzhin bzlas//mang na spyi'i phyag ni//re re la yang so sor bstan//de ltar byas na nges pa dang //byin gyis rlabs nas dngos
25a4(rgyud): grub 'byung //thal mo sbyar ba bkan pa las//'dzub mo gnyis ni cung zad bkug//'di ni de bzhin gshegs rigs kyis//dam tshig phyag rgyar shes par bya//thal mo legs par sbyar ba las//
25a5(rgyud): sor mo dbus ma gsum 'gyed pa//'di ni pad ma'i phyag rgya ste//pad ma'i kha phye ba'i mtshan nyid do//lag pa gnyis ni rgyab snol te//'the chung gnyis dang 'the bong gnyis//'di ni rdo rje'i phyag rgya ste//
25a6(rgyud): rdo rje'i rigs la bshad pa yin//dkyil 'khor rnams ni thams cad la//dam tshig gsum gyi phyag rgya rnams//rigs gsum dag las bshad pa ste//spyi'i yin pa rab tu sgrags//bsrung ba'i sa la phyogs cing
25a7(rgyud): dar//lha rnams la ni bskul ba dang //dbang bskur ba'i cho ga dang //dges rnams dag la bzlas pa dang //me tog la sogs 'bad pa dang //de bzhin gshegs rnams zhi bar bya//gdon rnams tshar
25a8(rgyud): bcad bya dang //bcing ba dang ni grol ba dang //phyag rgya phyag rgya'i le'u las ni//rgyas par yang dag par bshad pa yin//'di dag gi ni dkyil 'khor du//rnam par dbye ste las rnams bya//gas
25b1(rgyud): gang rnams sngar bshad pa//de dag thams cad rgyas byas te//srung ba dag laswogs pa la//'di ni cho ga bzhin du sbyar//gdon lasogs pa khyad par du//rdo rje tho bas 'tshams bcad do//gdon gyi dkyil 'khor 
25b2(rgyud): dag tu gang//phyag rgya bcwo brgya dang bshad pa'ang sbyar//rdo rje lcags kyus dgug pa'ang byas//bcing rdo rje lcags sgrog ste//rdo rje khu tshur 'dul ba la//rdo rje sder mo sdigs la sbyar//khro bo chen po stobs po che//
25b3(rgyud): dam tshig 'dzin du bzhug par sbyar//rigs sngags rgyal po mdzes pa ni//phyag rgya bcas la gnod rnams 'joms//bar du drag po'i gdon rnams la//rdo rje tho bas sbyar nas ni//brtson 'grus mthu 
25b4(rgyud): rtsal chen po ste//gcig pus kyang ni las rnams 'grub//gdod nas sbyar ba'i phyag rgya ni//ma lus pa dag 'bar 'gyur zhing//de dag gi mthu stobs kyang//der ni 
25b5(rgyud): lhags par mthong bar 'gyur//de nas gdon rnams char bcad par//phyag rgya dag kyang 'bad te sbyar//las la ji ltar mthong ba bzhin//gzhan dag la yang sbyar bar byed 
25b6(rgyud): de nas yam shing dag gis ni//sbar ba'i me ma sbyin sreg bya//shar du kha ltas ku shI //khros la 'dug ste mnyam gzhag ste//ku sha 'gram dang gsang rtor bya//g.yas phyogs dag nas skor
25b7(rgyud): zhing bya//mchod cing yam shing me sbar la//thog mar dang ni tha mar yang//mar gyi sregs lugs dgang lugs kyis//so so dag tu dbul bar bya//gong du bzlas brjod ji skad 
25b8(rgyud): bshad//sbyin sreg dag ni de bzhin sgrags//yang na re re'i phyed du lnga//zhi ba'i sngags kyis so sor sbyar//gsang sngags thams cad rims bzhin du//

26a8(rgyud): gos kyis gdong g.yogs te//sngar bstan pa'i cho ga la//me tog dag ni gzhag nas su//de nas dkyil 'khor bdag po'i sngags//phyag rgyar bcas pa'i spyi bor gzhag//gsang 
26b1(rgyud): sngags lan gsum bzlas byas nas//nyid kyis bde ba'i byin brlabs gsol//'di ltar bdag gi slob ma 'di//dkyil 'khor du ni legs btsud nas//lha'i rigs kyi rim pa dang //'di la bsod
26b2(rgyud): nams ci 'dra las//de bzhin du ni 'bab gyur cig//'dis ni dngos grub gang 'grub dang //rigs ni gang gi snod du 'gyur//bsod nams mthu ni ci yod pa//dkyil 'khor 'dir ni de bzhin 'gyur
26b3(rgyud): de skad brjod nas de 'og tu//de nas sgo'i phyogasu de//gzhag nas me tog dor tu bzhug//gang du me tog babs gyur pa//de ni de'i rigasu gsungs//de nas gdong g.yogs bkrol nas ni//
26b4(rgyud): dkyil 'khor bzang po stan par bya//de nas bla dga' 'gyur te//slob ma de la 'di skad sgo//dkyil 'khor bzang po 'di la ltos//da ni dang pa rab tu bskyed//khyod ni sangs rgyas riT
26b5(rgyud): su skyes//sngags dang rig pas byin gyis rlobs 'gro ba'i phun sum dngos grub kyang //thams cad khyod la mngon du 'gyur//dngos grub phyir ni dam tshig skyongs//gsang sngags
