26b7(rgyud): gyis//dkyil 'khor de ni rab sngags sam//snyed pa sbyin bya des kyang ni//mthong na dkyil 'khor gnyis pa zlos//dngos grub rang 'bring rnam gsum ni//me tog 'bab pa las rtag+sa
26b8(rgyud): te//lha rnams kyi ni khyad par ni//gnas kyi khyad par dag la rtag//gang tshe sangs rgyas bcom ldan gyi//dbul me tog babs gyur na//de tshe gtsug
27a1(rgyud): @#//rtor mdzad spu dag//gdon mi za bar 'grub par 'gyur//smad du babs par gyur na ni//dmag gzhug la sogs 'grub par 'gyur//stong du babs na mchog rnams 'grub//ro smad phyogs 
27a2(rgyud): su babs nas ni//dngos grub phal rnams 'grub par 'gyur//bar gyi sku la babs na ni//snying po la sogs 'grub par 'gyur //smad du babs par gyuran ni//dmag bzhug la sogs 'grub par
27a3(rgyud): 'gyur//rim des pad mo rdo rje yis//rigs kun la yang sgre bar bya//de bzhin lha gzhan rnams la yang //dngos grub rnam gsum du ni bshad//gal te lha'i gzugs dag las//gang du bkram 
27a4(rgyud): pa tshun chad ni//de yi ras ris de bzhin te//me tog de ltar de yi rigs//lha gnyis kyi n-i bar dag tu//gal te me tog babs gyur na//gang la nye bar gyur na ni//de ni de yi rigasub shad de//
27a5(rgyud): gal te la las me tog dag//dkyil 'khor dang po 'das ltung na//de la dngos grub phal 'grub cing //de ni dad pa rab tu chung //gal te me tog 'thor ba dag//ra ba'i mo la babs na//
27a6(rgyud): zhi ba byas nas de dag 'dod//nan tan bskyed de dor du bzhug//de ste the tsom gyur nas ni//mkhas pas bzlas te dor mi bzhug//der ni gar yang nges med par//der ni dngos grub thob mi 'gyur //
27a7(rgyud): gnyis kyi bar du mnyam pa dang //don med phyi ru ltung na ni//zhi ba'i sbyin sreg byas rnams kyis//de nas me tog dor du rtag//ji ltar gnas pa'i tshul dag las//rtso bo rnams kyis rigs 
27a8(rgyud): kyis skyes//shes rab pha rol phyin dbus sam//dkyil 'khor gyi ni gtso bo dang //sgo srungs gnyis la 'tshungs 'gyur na//de ltar snying po spyod yul legs//gal te me tog bor ba dag//
27b1(rgyud): mchod pa'i steng du 'bab 'gyur na//de ni rgyas pa'i sgrub pa dag//khyad par du ni shes par bya//dkyil 'khor gyi ni bdag po la//gang gi me tog 'bab 'gyur na//de ni de yi las rnams kun//myur du
27b2(rgyud): 'grub cing mchog tu 'gyur//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das la ni//gang gi me tog babs gyur na//de la rigs gsum bsang sngags kun//gdon mi za bar 'grub par 'gyur//pad ma 'dzin la rigs
27b3(rgyud): gny-is 'grub//yang na lha ni snyen pa bzhin//gsang sngags 'grub par shes par bya//gang gis gnyis dang gsum pa las//'das te song bar gyur na ni//dkyil 'khor de las 'byung ba
27b4(rgyud): bya//yang na dkyil 'khor gzhan du gzhag//de laswogs pa'i rnam pa yis//me tog rnams ni bor ba las//bdag gi mdun du gang ltung ba//de la zhi ba rtsam par bya//
27b5(rgyud): sbyin sreg gi ni zhi byas nas//der ni slar yang dor du bzhug//lan gsum spogs te babs na//re thag chad de slar rtong ngo//blo dang ldan pa'i cho ga 'dis//slob ma thaMd
27b6(rgyud): bzung bar bya//btsud nas me tog dri lasogs//dkyil 'khor du ni dbul bar bya//slob ma gcig nas bkug nasu//cho ga bzhin du yon phul te//cho ga shes pas gsang rtor bya//
27b7(rgyud): g.yon gyi phyogasu gzhag nas ni//de'-i lag g.yas the bong nas//lag pa g.yon par bzung nas ni//dkyil 'khor bdag po'-i gsang sngags kyis//mkhas pas sreg lugs bdun bdun
27b8(rgyud): dbul//de nas zh-i ba'i gsang sngags kyis//mar gyi sreg lugs lan bdun bya//de nas slob ma blugs bzar gyis//skor cing sreg lugs lan bdun bya //de nas srad bu bcings nas ni//
28a1(rgyud): @#//gsang rdor snga ma bzhin du byas nas//snying khar bram gyi sngags btab pa//ji ltar 'dod pa slar btang ngo//slob ma rnams ni thams cad la//cho ga 'di ni bshad pa yin//sbyin sreg las
28a2(rgyud): ni ba mar gyis//klugs bzar nyid kyis de bzhin bya//ku sha'i chu dang ku sha'i stan//de bzhin ku sha'i gsang phyed dang//ku sha bkram pa dag dang ni//sbyin sreg las la rab tu bya//mchod sbyin shing
28a3(rgyud): gi bud shing gi//ba mar skud pas me 'bar te//de la yam shing rlom sregs pas//sbyin sreg dag la de ltar sreg//mar gyi sbyin sreg ji snyed pa//til gyi sbyin sreg de snyed bya//'bras tshe ji
28a4(rgyud): ltar sregs nas ni//tha mar dgang blugs de ltar bya//me ni bod dang btang ba dang//rang gi gsang sngags dag gis bya//de'i gsang rdor las kun pa'i//gsang sngags dag gis
28a5(rgyud): bya ba yin//dkyil 'khor du ni sbyin sregs las//gsang sngags spyod pa gang yin pa//de dag gis ni cho ga bzhin//mkhas pas der ni sbyin sreg bya//de nas gzhan yang
28a6(rgyud): zhi ba dang //rgyas pa dang ni de bzhin du//drag shul spyod pa'i sbyin sreg las//khyad par du ni mkhas par bya//las dang ji ltar rjes 'thun par//dkyil 'khor cho ga bzhin du bris nas//mnyaM
28a7(rgyud): gzhag rdzas ni 'di dag gis//sbyin sreg las ni rab tu bya//byang du kha ltas zhi ba'i phyir//shar du kha ltas drag po ste//sbyin sreg las ni rtsam par bya//las rnams kun la kha ran te//zlum po
28a8(rgyud): dang ni gru bzhi dang//gru gsum yin te dkar po las//ser dang nag po gsum tu sgrags//zhi ba la ni pad ma'i stan//rgyas pa la ni ku sha'i khres//drag shul spyod la rtan med par//rkang pa
28b1(rgyud): rkang pas mnan te bya//las gsum dag la zhi ba dang //dga' dang de bzhin khro bo dang //mkhas pa yongasu ji skad du//'byung po cho ga de bzhin bya//zhi ba yam shing rtse mo ste//dbus kyis las la rgyas pa 
28b2(rgyud): ste//drag shul pa la rtsa ba ste//yam shing rnam pa gsum du bshad//zhi ba'i las la ku sha dang //lo mo laswogs gon nasu//rgyas par du gu laswogs pa//ras phran ser pos yongasu sgrags//mngon 
28b3(rgyud): spyod gos ni dkar po'o//khrag gis btab pa'i bya ba yin//yang na gos rnying dri ma can//bgo ba 'am spyir ni rtsam par bya//nas dang sgrang rtsi 'o ma dang //mar dang 'bras yos 'o thug dang //dur ba'i myu 
28b4(rgyud): gu kun da dang //u dum a shad pa la shad//de bzhin sham mya'i yam sreg shing //de la sogs pa thams cad ni//zhi ba'i sbyin sreg rdzas yin no//nas thug zho dang lag phan dang //'o ma 
28b5(rgyud): dang ni nas dkar can//ti li ni rnam pa gsum sbyar ba//sgron cing shu ti nye'u shing //nag pus pa bil ba'i rdzas//'bru rnams kun ni yam sreg shing //de laswogs pa'i rdzas rnams ni//
28b6(rgyud): rgyas pa'i sbyin sreg rdzas la bshad//yungs kar dang ni skye tshe dang //yungs dkar dmar dang de bzhin dug//rus che dud khrod thal ba dang //de bzhin sgra ni rtum po dang //ko po ke ra 'gal dum dang //
28b7(rgyud): seng ldeng dang ni a ri sta//yam shing tsher ma can la sogs//drag shul can gyi rdzasu bshad//sngags kyi rgyud la ma gsungs kyang //blo dang ldan pas rdzas 'di rnams//rab tu drag po'i las dag la//
28b8(rgyud): cho ga bzhin du sbyar bar bya//sbyin sreg cho ga byas nas ni//slob ma dbang skur 'o rigs nas//cho ga bzhin du nan tan bya//dbang skur ba'i cho ga bya//las kun byed pa'i gsang sngags kyis//btab 
29a1(rgyud): @#//pa'i stan ni gsar gzhag ste//bkra shis la sogs kun byas te//bsrung ba nan tan cher phul zhing //bla ma snyen bkur byas pa dag//stan la de ni gzhag nasu//sngags kyi skyon gsal bkra shis bya/
29a2(rgyud): bdug spos legs par sbyar ba yi//bdug pa khro bo'i rgyal pos bdug//rnga yab laswogs bzung ba dang //spyi bo'i steng du gdugs kyang bzung //rnga ma'i gsil yab gdug spos dang //zhwa dang gos kyi slob ma
29a3(rgyud): dag//'di dag dang ni de bzhin du//bum pa bzhi yang bshams te dogs//shin tu bkra shis tshigs bcad sgrogs//de bzhin bkra shis rgya cher gnon//'byor par gyur pa'i sil snyan dang //klu dbyangs dag
29a4(rgyud): dang rnga yab sgras//'dor na de laswogs pa yi//cho ga rgyas par byas nasu//yid kyis dkyil 'khor lha kun la//mkhas pas gsol ba btab nas ni//bum pa'i bzlas brjod rgyar
29a5(rgyud): byas pa//de ni legs par blangs nasu//dkyil 'khor dal bus lan gsum skor//rtsa ba'i gsang sngags kun 'don cing //blo dang ldan pas dbang skur bya//de la phyag rgya bcings nasu//
29a6(rgyud): gsang sngags zlos shing spyi bor 'dug//gsang sngags rgyud ni 'dzin gyur pa//blo dang ldan pas dbangaskur ro//slob ma yon tan kun ldan pa//nub phyogs kha ltas cho ga bya//byang phyogs kha bltas zhi ba'i
29a7(rgyud): phyir//rgyas pa'i cho ga shar du ltas//drag shul spyod pa de bzhin du//lho phyogs kha ltas brtson de bya//de ltar dbang skur byas nas ni//de las gsang ba'i gos rnams gon//metog dri lasogs pas mchod
29a8(rgyud): mchod phyir thogs dang rdo bur bcas//de nas bla mas bdag nyid kyis//de yi spyi bor gdugs kyang bzung //thal mo sbyar bar byas nas ni//dkyil 'khor legs par bkod du bzhug//gdugs dang
29b1(rgyud): bcas pa sgo phyogasu //spyi bo 'dud cing phyag 'tshal bzhug//gsang sngags rgyud kun 'dzin 'gyur pa yi//slob ma 'di ni 'bul ba lags//mkhan pos de skad smras nasu//'dun gzhag nas ni slob ma de//lag 
29b2(rgyud): pas legs par blangs nasu//de nas 'di skad sgo bar bya//de ni dkyil 'khor slob dpon 'gyur//gsang sngags rgyud ni 'dzin par 'gyur//sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' dang //lha rnams kyang ni 
29b3(rgyud): kun gyis bkur//sems can rnams la rjes rje ba'i//khyod kyis dkyil 'khor cho ga bzhin//nan tan bskyed te bri bar byos//sgrub po pa rnams sngags la sbyor//de skad sgos nas de 'og tu//sbyin
29b4(rgyud): sreg cho ga sngon bzhin bya//yam sreg shing gi me la ni//dkyil 'khor bdag po'i gsang sngags kyis//mar gyi sbyin sreg rgya dag bya//de bzhin zhi ba'i gsang sngags kyis//zho dang sbrang 
29b5(rgyud): rtsi mar sbyar ba'i//til gyi sreg lugs rgya yang dbul//sbyin sreg zin nas spyi bor ni//chu de nyid kyis gsang rtor bya//de nas de la dkyil 'khor ni//rab tu rgyas par bstan par 
29b6(rgyud): bya//phyag rgya chen po dag kyang bstan//rig sngags rgyal po klog tu bzhug//de nas slob ma blo ldan pas//metog snyim pa bkang nasu//bdag gis sngags dang lha rnams la//go rims bzhin du ma ch+wo 
29b7(rgyud): byas nas//slob dpon sbyin sreg gnas 'dug la//rga shing gus pas dran byas la//de tshe bla ma 'dod pa yi//yo na ni phyag rgyas dbul bar bya//bdag dang bdog pa kun bcas par//slob dpon 
29b8(rgyud): la ni dbul bar bya//bdag nyid dbang po'i skye bo kun//nor dang bcas shing rkang 'bros bcas//gzhan yang nor gyis 'gu ba dang //yang na gang la dga' yang rung //yang na bdag la ci bdog pa//
30a1(rgyud): @#//bla ma la ni yon dbul lo//yang na dbul pos bla ma la//bkur sti dag gis mgu bar bya//slob dpon dag ni thams cad la//bkur sti bya ba yin par bshad//dang po ras zung cig phul nas//phyi nas
30a2(rgyud): chog 'di kun bya//slob ma gsang sngags sgrub 'dod pas//bla ma mchod pa bya bar rigs//slob ma dam tshig bsrung ba yi//gos dang gser dang bdag nyid dang //ba dang bdog pa thams cad ni//bla ma dag la yon
30a3(rgyud): du dbul//de nas bla mas slob ma rnams//ma yengs par ni bkod nasu//shes rab pha rol phyin blangs te//dam tshig 'di ni sgo bar bya//de ring phyin chad khyod rnams kyis//sangs rgyas chos dang dge
30a4(rgyud): 'dun dang //rig sngags gsang sngags tshogs rnams la//dad pas rab tu rtan par bya//rtag par phyag rgya chen po dang //khyad par du ni mos par bya//dam tshig can dang 'dza'o dang //
30a5(rgyud): bla ma la yang gus par bya'o//lha rnams kun la dad mos dang //dus mtshams dag tu mchod par bya//ston pa gzhan gyi gzhung mi chod//rtag tu blo bur 'gron mchod bya//srog chags
30a6(rgyud): rnams la byams pa'i sems//rab tu rtan par nye bar gzhag/theg pa che la dga' rnams kyis//bsod nams dag la nan tan skyed//zlas brjod byed pa'abad pa yis//gsang sngags spyad pa 
30a7(rgyud): rtson par bya//gsang sngags rgyud las stan pa yis//dam tshig rnams kyang bsrung bar bya//dam tshig med pa rnams la ni//sngags dang phyag rgya mi sbyin no//gsang sngags rgyud ni legs bsrung zhing //
30a8(rgyud): de yang bdag gis rtor bar bya//de ltar dam tshig sgos nas ni//nyid kyi phyag rgya rnams stan la//nyid kyi rigs kyi rig pa sbyin//dkyil 'khor dag kyang bshad par bya//de nas kha lag bkrus
30b1(rgyud): nas ni//lha la mchod yon phul nas ni//go rims bzhin du thams cad la//bdug spos cho ga bzhin du phul//de nas dkyil 'khor rig sngags kyis//sbyin sreg ci tsam 'dod pa bya//de nas phyogs
30b2(rgyud): kyi rtor ma sbyin//snga ma sh-ing kha lag yang bkrus nas//me tog dri la swogs pa yi//slob mas ci ltar 'dod pa mchod//slob ma dang bcas ma yengs par//mgo bos gus pas phyag 'tshal te
30b3(rgyud): srod du bshad pa gang yin pa//de nas spos chus phul nasu//so so'i sngags kyis ci rigs par//de nas slar ni gshegasu gsol//swa ma rnams ni chags nasu//mtsho chen nang du dor bar bya//bza'
30b4(rgyud): ba dang ni bca' ba rnams//ci dgar 'phongs pa rnams la sbyin//khyi dang bya rog laswogs la//cung zad tsam yang mi sbyin no//yo byad rdzas rnams thams cad ni//slob dpon gyis ni
30b5(rgyud): spyad par bya//slob ma rnams la go rims bzhin slob ma rnams la rgor mi rung//dkyil 'khor gyi ni yo byad kun//slob dpon bdag nyid dbang bar bshad//de nas slob mas bshad
30b6(rgyud): nas ni//dam tshig las ni nyams par 'gyur//gang zhig spyod par mi 'dod na//'di yi cho ga'i rim pa 'dis//dkon mchog gsum gyi cho ga yo byad ni//de dag cho ga bzhin du dbul//gdugs dang
30b7(rgyud): rnga yab dril bu swogs//sangs rgyas nyid la dbul bar bya//bden dang gdug spos dr-i laswogs//dam pa'i chos la dbul bar bya//gos dang yo byad snod laswogs//dge 'dun la yang dbul bar bya//
30b8(rgyud): de dag la ni slob pa yis//cung zad tsam yang mi kur ro//dge 'dun med na dge slong sbyin//yang na dge slong ma la sbyin//gal te bdag nyid mi 'dod na//dge snyen dag la sbyin par bya//de nas
31a1(rgyud): @#//de yi phyi de nyin//nyes byas lhag ma zhi ba'i phyir //mkhas pas yis ni zhi ba yi//sbyin sreg rgya rtsa brgyad bya'o//de nas gzhan yang rgyud kun la //dkyil 'khor ji ltar bshad pa yi//cho ga lhag
31a2(rgyud): ma lus pa//'dir ni lhag ma rab tu bshad//slob dpon gsang sngags rgyud la mkhas//snying rje'i bdag nyid tshul khrims ldan//dkyil 'khor bri ba la mkhas shing //drang la rtsa sgra che ba shis//khang stong
31a3(rgyud): rdo thal gyis byugs la//rtsang ma bzang po spangs pa dag//lha khang du yang dkyil 'khor ni//ji skad bshad pa lags pa bri//ci 'tshol chu la sbyar ba yi//khang khyim byed pa de bzhin du//chu stongs spang leb bzang po 
31a4(rgyud): yis//gtams pa dag tu bri bar bya//mchod sbyin sa dang grong gi sa//mi rtsang ba'i sa gang dang//gnang yang dkyil 'khor du bshad pa'i//sa ni 'bad te spang bar bya//la las dkyil
31a5(rgyud): 'khor bdag po ni//gcig pu kho nar las sbyar sgrags;de nyid kyis ni bya ba ste//sa 'og dkyil 'khor lta bu yin//kha cig tu dkyil 'khor tshogs kyis ni//las rnams bya ba dbang du bshad//
31a6(rgyud): der yang de nyid kyis byas te//khro mo dkyil 'khor lta bu yin //gang du las 'di yod gyur na//rang gi las kyi cho ga med//der ni mkhas pas spyi rgyud las//gsungs pa'i las rnams byas yin//dkyil
31a7(rgyud): 'khor du ni dgod pa'i sngags//gang dag yongasu sgrags pa'i bsngags//thog ma de ni re re la//zlas brjod khri khri bya bar bshad//mkhas pas dkyil 'khor gang 'di ba//de dag rgyas par bzlas na ni//dran pa'i
31a8(rgyud): bya ba'i phyir yang ni//klog pa de yangs nye bar gzhag//khrus laswogs pa byed pa'i tshe//dkyil 'khor mthong ba gzhan gzhag ste//dkyil 'khor bri ba'i tshe na ni//nam yang stong par mi gzhag go//
31b1(rgyud): thig 'debs pa las babs pa'i tshe//slob ma gal te med na yang//der ni phur bu'i cho ga yis//bdag rang nyid kyis thig btab po//dkyil 'khor dag gi las dag la //gal te grogs shig med
31b2(rgyud): na yang//der ni mkhas pas bdag nyid kyis//rnam pa de bzhin las byaso//nad bu can dang tshul nyams dang//rtsang sgra chung zhing ma rlabs pa//de lta bu dag rgyud mthong na//gragasu bzung bar mi
31b3(rgyud): bya'o//bder gshegs chos ni mi byed pa//la la rigs btsun dad cing drad//dkyil 'khor 'dir ni dad gyur te//gal te khral du 'ongs nas ni//de y-i dang brtan shes nas//de lta bu dge
31b4(rgyud): dkyil 'khor dag ni bris nas kyang//bdag gi stan la zhugs pa'i phyir//de ni cig pu gzhug par bya//de'i sta gnas pa yang//de dag nyid ni mod la mthong//slob ma
31b5(rgyud): nang mi mthun pa dang//cho ga'-i tshul ni med pa las//ji skad bshad ba'i zhi ba bya//rgyal po'-i ming ni snying rje can//lag na mtshon thogs pas srung zhing//sa'i
31b6(rgyud): bdag pos gnang na ni//slob dpon dkyil 'khor bri bar bya//slob ma yongasu bzung ba gang//sto gan gnas pa byas pa las//gal te rkyen gyis ma yongs na//bla ma blo
31b7(rgyud): ldan de'i gzugs rtan byas nasu//ji skad bshad pa'i las rnams bya//slob ma yongasu bzung pa dang//gal te rkyen g+ha zhig gis ni//der ni 'ongs par ma gyur na//gzhan dag li ni sbyin par gzhug
31b8(rgyud): sta gon gnas pa byas pa las//gal te rkyen gyis ma yong na//bla ma blo ldan mkhas pi yis//der ni slob ma gzhan mi zung//sta gon gnas par bya ba la//bla ma gal te dkyil
32a1(rgyud): @#//'khor du//gal te 'ongs par ma gyur na//nad kyis btab na nges par 'chi//rkyen 'ga' zhig gi skabs nasu//gal te nyin cig bar chad ni//de ni nyin par zhi byas la//slar yang sta go na 
32a2(rgyud): gnas par bya//yo byad ces par ma 'byor kyang//shol ba dag kyang yong mi bya//gal te gzhol na bla ma la//gnod pa mang po 'byung bar 'gyur//dkyil 'khor bri ba'i tshe na yeng//gnod pa 'ga' zhig 
32a3(rgyud): 'byung 'gyur na//bshad pa'i sngags kyi stobs kyis su//de ni slar yang zlog par bya//des kyang zlog par ma nus na//mchod pa'i yo byad thams cad ni//gsang rtor byas nas phul nasu//mchod yon phul
32a4(rgyud): slar gshegasu gsol//blo dang ldan pas phyi de nyin//dkyil 'khor snga ma bzhin du bri//yang na mkhas pas sbyin sreg gi//las kyis zhi bar du bya//ma mo bduni gyi dkyil 'khor dang
32a5(rgyud): gnod sbyin dkyil 'khor bshad pa gang//de la swogs pa dgod pa ni//nyes pa rims bzhin rtag mi dgos//de dag du ni de bzhin du//dkyil 'khor bkod pa rgyas par bya//cho ga byas
32a6(rgyud): pa gzhan dag la//cho ga 'di ni ci rigs bya//mdor bsdus bshad pa gang yin pa'i//rgyas par bshad pa gang yin dang//zur gyi stan dang mod la ni//spyir stan pa ni gang yin pa//de rnams bya ba'i cho ga la//
32a7(rgyud): rgyud ni rgyas bshad pa yin//mkhas pas cho ga 'di yis n-i//dkyil 'khor thams cad bri bar bya//dkyil 'khor chen po 'od las ni//bshad pa'i cho ga rgyas par bshad//bla mas ji skad stan pa dang//
32a8(rgyud): rtog pa dag la ci gsungs pa//gsang sngags kun gyi 'dul ba las//cho ga rim pa ci bshad p+sa//dkyil 'khor gyi ni cho ga las//mkhas pas cho ga de bzhin sbyar//gsang sngags kyi ni
32b1(rgyud): tshad dag la //bum pa laswogs bstan byas la//der yang cho ga 'di yis ni //lhag par byas pa nyes pa med//de bzhin gsang sngags so cho ga las//lha gzhan gyi ni rtor ma yang //dkyil
32b2(rgyud): 'khor dag tu bshad pa las //lhag mar byas dang nyes pa med//de bas gang du cung zad cig//klad de bshad pa gang yin pa//de ni dgos pa med sems kyis//thams cad de bzhin kho nar bya//
32b3(rgyud): la lar slob mar dang bcas te//bdug spos dang ni mar me dang //lag tu thogs te mnyam par ni//dkyil 'khor ba bskor ba bshad pa dang //dam tshig ston tshe slob ma rnams//las kyi grur ni zhugs nasu//
32b4(rgyud): me tog gtor bar bya'o zhes//khyad par bshad pa gang yin pa//de laswogs pa'i cho ga dag//gang du cung zad bshad pa yin der ni 'ga' mgu nyid 'gyur ba'i//kun la de kun sbyar te
32b5(rgyud): dgos//lha'i rigs rnams mthong ba dang //dbang skur ba yi rnam pa yi//bla ma mnyam par gzhag pa yis//de dag shes nas ci rigs bya//slob dpon go 'phangs thob
32b6(rgyud): pa'i phyir//dang po yongasu sgrags pa yin//rigasngags pa yin rnams ni sgrub bya'i phyir//gnyis pa legs par bshad pa yin//bgegs rnams du ma gzhom pa'i phyir//gsum pa rab tu sgrags
32b7(rgyud): pa yin//bzhi pa 'byor pa thob pa'i phyir//cho ga rgyas pa des bshad do//dang po cho ga rgyas pa ni//nga yis thog mar bshad pa yin//rigs gsum dbang skur cho ga yang //mdor bsdus
32b8(rgyud): nas ni 'di bshad do//slob ma'i lha rigs mthong ba//rigasngags gang la bzlo 'dod pa//de yis brgyad brgya bzlos pa yis//bum pas mngon par dbang skur bya//de nyid kyis
33a1(rgyud): @#//kyang sbyin sreg kyang//de nyid du yang zlas nasu //de ltar gang zhig dbang skur ba//dbang skur cho ga (gnyis )yin//sems ni bgegs kyis brlabs pa dag//zhi bar bya ba gang yin pa //de ni dbang skur
33a2(rgyud): gsum pa ste//phan pa dang bde ba thob par bshad //dpal la sogs pa'i dkyil 'khor du//rgyal srid thob phyir dbang skur ba//byur ni med par bya ba dang//dbang yang bskyed par bya ba dang//nor dang
33a3(rgyud): bu tsha phan pa dang//de la sogs pa dbang skur gang//de ni zhi ba'i ming rnams la//'byor pa sbyin par shes par bya//dbang skur dang po thob pa ni//sangs rgyas rnams kyis dgongs par 'gyur//des ni
33a4(rgyud): gsang sngags spyod pa la//brtson pa rnams kyis bkur bar 'gyur//rigasngags gsang sngags mang po dang//'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang gis//dpa'o rdo rje 'dzin dag//
33a5(rgyud): nyid kyis byin gyis rlabs 'gyur ba//de yis sbyar nas sgrub pa pos//las rnams thams cad sgrub par 'gyur//des ni cis kyang mi tshugs shing//lha rnams kyis kyang mchod par
33a6(rgyud): 'gyur//'khor ba na ni 'khor ba na//de ni ngan song ltung mi 'gyur//yan lag nyams dang dbul ba dang//snod pa rnamasu skye mi 'gyur//tshe rabs dran zhing longs spyod che//tshul khrims ldan zhing
33a7(rgyud): gzugs kyang bzang//rtag tu lha dang mi rnams kyi//mtho ris gnasu skye bar 'gyur//de yi bsod nams mthu yis ni//sangs rgyas byang chub las mi ldog//de ni ring po mi thogs par//
33a8(rgyud): byang chub mchog 'di rdzogs par 'gyur//de laswogs pa'i yon tan ni//mang po rgya chen dag dang ldan//rigasngags rnams kyi rigs skye zhing//des ni byang chub spyad pa spyod//de ltar
33b1(rgyud): mngon par dbang skur zhing //blo ldan rgyud 'di mthong ba la//bla ma la ni lung thob pas//dkyil 'khor dag ni bri bar bya//yon tan 'di dang mi ldan par//gang zhig dkyil 'khor 'bri byed pa//
33b2(rgyud): de ni mi bzad dmyal bar ltung //slob ma rnams kyi don med 'gyur//mu ge dang ni nad mang dang //than pa dang ni 'tshe ba 'byung //rgyal po rnams kyang 'thab pa dang //tshom rkun laswogs 'jigs pa 
33b3(rgyud): 'byung //de dang de'i rgyun rnams kyis//slob rnams kyang phung bar 'gyur//de bzhin gshegs pa'i dkyil 'khor dang //dkyil 'khor cho ga ma tshang bar//bris na bla ma nges par mchi//gang zhig
33b4(rgyud): legs par dbang skur ba//de ni rigs rnams gsum char gyis//dkyil 'khor laswogs slob dpon 'gyur//'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang gis//dkyil 'khor du ni 
33b5(rgyud): dbang skur//ma mo gnod sbyin gsang sngags kyis//dkyil 'khor gang dag ci bshad pa//thams cad de dag kun gyis kyang //dgos pa med par bri bar bya//bla mas
33b6(rgyud): slob ma cig pu ni//dkyil 'khor dag tu dbang skur bya//mkhas pas cig char slob ma gnyis//dbang skur ba ni yod mi bya//gnyis sam gsum 'am kha zhi yang rung //bla mas
33b7(rgyud): dbang rab bskur ba ni//yo byad gsang ba gzhan rnams kyis//thams cad so sor bya//rigasngags dbang ni thob pa la//sgrub pa'i dkyil 'khor dbang skur bya//dbang skur ba'i
33b8(rgyud): cho ga dang //sgrub pa'i cho ga de bzhin sbyin//slob ma dam tshig shes pa ni//gzhan rnams la yang rang gi sngags//cho gar bcas shing phyag rgyar bcas//cho ga rgyas dang
34a1(rgyud): @#//bcas pa sbyin//dkyil 'khor legs par ma mthong bar//gang zhig ci ste sngags bzlos na//de ni dngos grub thob mi 'gyur//shi na ngan song ltung bar 'gyur//sngags dang phyag rgya cho ga 
34a2(rgyud): la//gang zhig de la ston pa yang//de ni dam tshig nyams 'gyur na//du 'obad sems can dmyal bar 'gro//ji skad bshad pa'i dam tshig rnams//gal te nyams par gang 'gyur ba//de ni rang gi 
34a3(rgyud): snying po sngags//gcig 'bum du ni bzlas brjod bya//yang na sems rmongs med pa'i gzugs//stong du bzlas brjod byas na yang//yang na bzhi ba'i sbyin sreg bya//yang dkyil 'khor du yang bzhug
34a4(rgyud): gti mug dbang gis mi shes pa'i//mi gang dkyil 'khor 'bri byed pa'i//de ni 'tshams med lnga'i las//'bra ba'i gnasu 'gro bar 'gyur//gang zhig bsod nams las byed pa 
34a5(rgyud): cho ga legs par byas nas ni//byang chub sems dpa' mthu chen dang//'dod pa yi ni dam par skye//gshed byed rnams dang ro langs dang//sha za lto 'phye chen po dang//rnam pa sna
34a6(rgyud): tshogs rdon rnams 'jigs//par gyur pa'i bgegs rnams kyis//lha rnams rigs mthong dam tshig shes//rtag tu tshugs par mi 'gyur zhing//de yi mi dge las rnams ni//ngan song du yang 
34a7(rgyud): yod mi 'gyur//de yis sgrubs te nges par ni//gsang sngags thams cad 'grub par 'gyur//de la byang chub sa bon ni//rten par 'gyur ba dgos pa med//slob dpon blo gros chen po yis//gsang sngags
34a8(rgyud): rgyud ni 'dzin pa dag//slob ma rngams pa'i snying rje can//gcig ni nan tan bskyed te bsgrub//dkyil 'khor rnams ni thams cad kyi//cho ga 'di ni dam pa ste//blo dang ldan pa'i cho ga 'dis//dkyil
34b1(rgyud): 'khor rnams ni bri bar bya//rigasngags rgyal dang phyag rgya che//dkyil 'khor rnams ni thams cad dag//slob ma la ni sngags byin nas//de nas bla mas rgyud 'di byin nas//de nas bla ma rgyud
34b2(rgyud): dbang gi dkyil 'khor gshegasu gsol//sgrub pa chen po bya ba'i bshad lugs kyis//dkyil 'khor mdzad byad ma lus pa//yongasu rdzogs par bstan pa yin no//dkyil 'khor chen po
34b3(rgyud): rnam par phye ba yi//rim pa phye ba phyag na rdo rje dbang phyug chen pos bstan pa las//dkyil 'khor thams cad kyi spyi'i cho ga rdzogs s+h+yo////@##////rgya gar skad du//
34b4(rgyud): sarba ta thA ga tA Sh+Thi sha na badzra ya//na ma d+ha ra ni ka ba//kal pa sa hi sta//bod skad du//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi gtsug tor rnam par rgyal ba zhes pa'i gzungs rtog pa dang 
34b5(rgyud): bcas pa//sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshalo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa'i dus gcig na//bcom ldan 'das dpag tu med pa de bzhin gshegs pa 
34b6(rgyud): dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas //bde ba can gyi chos yang dag par bsdud pa'i phug//khangs zangs mchog na nye bar bzhugaso//byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 'phag+sa
34b7(rgyud): pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug la bka' stsal ba//rigs kyi bu sdug bsngal ba nad kyis yongasu gzir ba'i sems can tshe thung ba yod te//de rnams kyi phyir 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor la nye bar gzigs 
34b8(rgyud): te//kun tu gzigs pa'i dpal gyi mtshan//de bzhin gshegs ba thams cad kyi gtsug tor ranam par rgyal ba zhes bya ba'i gzungs 'di bcang ba dang/klag pa dang/gzhan dag la rgya cher 
35a1(rgyud): /yang dag par ston pa dang /tshe ring ba sgrub pa bya ba'i phyed du'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug stan las langs te//thal mo sbyar
35a2(rgyud): bar gyur pas//bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das bstan du gsol //bder gshegs pa thams cad kyi gtsug rtor rnam par rgyal ba zhes bya ba'i gzungs //bde bar gsheg+sa
35a3(rgyud): ba thams cad kyi gtsug tor rnam par rgyal ba zhes ba ba'i gzungs //bde ba gshegs pas bstan du gsol /de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor la 
35a4(rgyud): gzigs te//kun tu gzigs pa'i dpal zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas//de bzhin gshegs pa thaMd kyi gtsug tor rnam bar rgyal ba zhes bya ba'i gsungs 'di
35a5(rgyud): bka' stsal pa//na mo b+ha ga wa te//sarba tre lo kya tra ti pi shiSh+Ta ya//b+hud d+ha ya te na ma//tad+ya thA//oM b+hu~M b+hu~M b+hu~M/sho d+ha ya sho d+ha ya/bi sho d+ha ya bi sho d+ha ya/a sa ma sa man ta/
35a6(rgyud): a ba b+ha sa swa ra Na ga te/ga ga na swa b+ha wa bi shud d+he/a b+hi shin tsa tu maM/sarba ta thA ga tA/su ga ta ba ra ba tsa na a mri te/a b+hi she kA+er ma hA mu tra/man tra ba d+le//a ha ra a ha ra/ma ma a yus
35a7(rgyud): san d+ha ra ni/sho d+ha ya sho d+ha ya//bi sho d+ha ya bi shau d+ha ya/ga ga na swa b+ha wa/bi shud d+he/uSh+Ni Sha bi dza ya/pa ri shud d+he swa hA shra ra smi san tso di te/sarba ta thA ga ta a bA lo ki te/Sha Ta pa ra mi ta pa ri
35a8(rgyud): pu ra ni/sarba ta thA ga ta ma tA+e/da sha b+hu mi pra tiSh+Thi te/sarba ta thA ga tA//hri da ya a d+hiSh+ThA na te a d+hiSh+Tha te/mu tre mu dre ma hA mu tre/badzra ka ya saM ha ta na pa ri shud d+he/sarba karmA a ba ra na bi shud+de
35b1(rgyud): pra ti ni par ta ya/ma ma a yur b+hi shud d+he/sarba ta thA ga tA sa ma ya/a d+hi Sh+Tha na a d+hi Sh+Thi te/oM mu ni mu ni/mahA mu ni/bi mu ni bi mu ni/mahA bi mu ni/ma ti ma ti mahA ma ti/ma ma ti/
35b2(rgyud): su ma ti/ta thA ga tA /b+hud d+ha/kro taM pa ri shud d+he/bi s+phu Ta/b+hud d+he/shud d+he/he he dza ya dza ya/bi dza ya bi dza ya/sma ra sma ra/s+pha ra s+pha ra/s+phA ra ya s+phA ra ya/sarba b+hud d+ha a d+hi Sh+Tha na a 
35b3(rgyud): d+hi Sh+Tha te/shud d+he shud d+he/b+hud d+he b+hud d+he/badzre badzre /ma badzre/su badzre/badzra ga b+he dza ya ga b+he/bi dza ya ga b+he/badzra dz+wa la gar+b+he/badzra ud pa be/badzra sa b+ha we/badzre badzri naM/
35b4(rgyud): badzra b+ha ban+tu ma ma sha ri raM/sarba satwan na tsa/ka ya pa ri shud d+hir b+ha wan tu/satwa me/sarba ga ti ba ri shud d+hi Sh+tsa/swarba ta thA ga tA Sh+tsa maM/sarba sa twa sh+tsa/sam shwa/sa
35b5(rgyud): yan tu/b+hud d+ha ya b+hud d+ha ya/sa d+hi ya sad+hi ya/b+ho d+ha ya b+ho d+ha ya/bi b+ho d+ha ya bi b+ho d+ha ya/mo tsa ya mo tsa ya/bi mo tsa ya bi mo tsa ya/sho d+ha ya sho d+ha ya/bi sho d+ha ya bi sho d+ha ya
35b6(rgyud): sa man ta mo tsa ya/sa man ta ra smi pa ri shud d+he/sarba ta thA ga tA hrid ya/a d+hi Sh+Tha na a d+hi Sh+Thi te/oM mu tre mu tre/man+ta pa dA+e swAhA////'phags pa gtsug tor rnam par rgyal
35b7(rgyud): ba zhes bya ba'i gzungs rdzogs s+h+yo////lha'i dbang po gtsug tor rnam par rgyal ba zhes bya ba'i gzungs 'di//ngan 'gro thams cad yongasu sbyong ba/sdig pa'i las kyi sgrib pa
35b8(rgyud): thams cad nam par sbyong ba/bde 'gro'i lam du 'gro ba/ngan 'gro thams cad rnam par 'jig par byed pa 'di ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das gang ga'o klungig 'bu phrag bye ba brgya bcu rtsa 
36a1(rgyud): @#//brgyad kyi bye ma snyed kyis gsungs pa/byin gyis brlabs dang/rjesu yi rang bar mdzad pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi ye shes kyi phyag rgyas btab ste/sems can thams cad
36a2(rgyud): bde bar 'gro bar bya ba dang/ngan 'gror ltung ba rnams zlog pa dang /sems can dmyal ba thams cad dang/dud 'gro'i skye gnas dang gshin rje'i 'jig rten la sogs pa sdug bsngal gyi gnas thams cad du/
36a3(rgyud): sdug bsngal bzhi nyams par 'gyur ba rnams dang /sdug sngal gyi rgya mtshor ltung ba'i sems can rnams/rnam par grol ba'i phyir gsungaso//de bzhin du sems can tshe thung zhing skal pa chung ba dang/smad pa
36a4(rgyud): dang rnam pa sna tshogs pa'i skye bar ltung ba rnams dang /bsam pa nyams pa dang/sems can lam nyams pa rnams kyi don du/gzungs 'di 'dzam bu'i kling du sems can rnams la/
36a5(rgyud): gzungs 'di dang phyag rgya dang/sngags yongasu rtad pa yin no//de'i phyir lha'i dbang po khyed kyis yang dag par bzung bar bya'o//lha'i dbang po gzungs 'di lan cig thos ma thag tu;

36a7(rgyud): yid tags kyi yul dang/lha ma yin gyi ris kyi lha dang/'di lta ste/gnod sbyin dang/srin po dang 'byung po dang/sha za dang sprul po dang/lus sprul po dang/rjed byed dang khyi dang /rus sbal dang

36b1(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa dang 'phrad pa dang/byang chub sems dpa'i rigasu skye ba 'am/rigs chen por skye ba ni 'di lta ste/bram ze'i rigs shing sa la chen po lta bu dang/rgyal rigs
36b2(rgyud): shing sa la chen po lta bu'i ma/khyim bdag gi rigs shing sa la chen po lha bu 'am/tshong dpon gyi rigs shing sa la chen po lnga bur skye'o//zhes nga smra'o//lha'i dbang po ltar na gzungs 'di'i mthus
36b3(rgyud): byang chub kyi snying po'i mthar thug gi bar tu skye ba yongasu dag pa chen po thob ste/lha'i dbang po 'di ltar gzungs 'di ni 'di ltar mthu che ba/phan yon che ba/stobs che ba/dge ba shis te/
36b4(rgyud): lha'i dbang po sems can rnams kyi don gyi phyir ro//gtsug tor rnam par rgyal ba zhes bya ba'i gzungs/ngan 'gro thaMd yongasu sbyong ba 'di gzhago//dber na nor bu rin po che nyi ma'i
36b5(rgyud): snying po shin tu dri ma med pa namakha' ltar yongasu dag pa 'od gsal ba/ni 'od zer gyi snang bas 'bar te 'dugo/lha'i dbang po de bzhin du sems can de 'ang gos pa med par
36b6(rgyud): shes par bya'o//'di lta ste dper na/'dzam bu'i kling chu bo'i gser/shin tu dri ma med pa shin tu yongasu dag pa/shin tu gnyen pa/shin tu sdug pa'o/lha'i dbang po de bzhin
36b7(rgyud): sems can de yang shin tu dag par rig par bya'o//tshe rabs gzhan du yang de bzhin du skye bar 'gyur ro//lha'i dbang po gang na gzungs 'di ston pa dang/bye brag tu ni yi ge 'drir 'jug
36b8(rgyud): pa dang/'dzin pa dang/klog pa dang/kha ton du byed pa dang/mchod pa dang kun chub par byed pa dang/nyan pa dang 'dzin par 'gyur ba/der 'gro ba thad yongasu dag par 'gyuro//seMn
37a1(rgyud): @#//dmyal ba thams cad rgyun chad par 'gyuro//lha'i dbang po gzungs 'di yi ger bris te/rgyal mtshan gyi rtse mor gzhag par bya/ri mthon po 'am/khang pa 'thon po 'am/mchod
37a2(rgyud): rten gyi nang du gzhag par bya'o//lha'i dbang po dge slong ngam dge slong ma 'am/dge snyen nam dge snyen ma 'am/de las gzhan pa'i rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo gang rgyal mtshan gyi rtse mo la 
37a3(rgyud): btags te gzhag pa mthong ngam/de dang nye bar gnas par gyur na/de'i grib mas phog pas/de'i rdul rlung gis btab pa phog na yang/lha'i dbang po sems can de la sdig pa thaMd dang/

37a7(rgyud): mar me dang byug pa dang/gdugs dang rgyal mtshan dang/ba dan dang /spos dang/rgyan rnams kyis brgyan par byed pa dang/lam gyi bzhi mthor mchod rten byas te/gzungs 'di gzhag la
37a8(rgyud): thal mo sbyar ba 'am phyag 'tshal ba 'am/skor ba byed pa lta ci smos te/lha'i dbang po sems can de dag gi//sems can chen po yin par rig par bya'o//de bzhin gshegs pa'i srasu rig par
37b1(rgyud): bya'o//chos kyi gnasu rig par bya'o//de bzhin gshegs pa'i mchod rten du rig par bya'o//de nas chos kyi rgyal po gshin rje de'i nam langs nas/bcom ldan 'das ga la ba de'i song ste//
37b2(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das la lha'i me tog dang gos dang/rgyan la swogs phas mchod pa dang/sti bstang byas nas des bcom ldan 'das kyis zhabs la btud nas/'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das
37b3(rgyud): gas zungs 'di ni mthu che zhing phan yon che ba lagaso//bcom ldan 'das bdag la rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad par sems can de'i slad du/'brang zhing kun tu srung ba dang/yongs su skyab pa'i slad du

37b5(rgyud): bgyid lagaso//de nas bcom ldan 'das la lan gsum skor ba bgyis te/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces bkA stsal to//bcom ldan 'das gzungs 'di'i cho ga zhim mo dang

37b8(rgyud): byas te/lan stong bzlas nas des sems can tshe thung ba de yang phyir nur ro//nad pa nad dang bral bar 'gyuro//sgrib pa thams cad rnam par dag par thob par 'gyuro//sems can
38a1(rgyud): @#//dmyal ba la sogs pa ngan 'gro thams cad las yongasu thar bar 'gyuro//tha na dud 'gro'i skye gnasu song ba'i srog chags bya'i rna lam du yang/gzungs 'di'i sgra sgrag par byas te/
38a2(rgyud): de nas de ni ngan 'gro de nyid ma yin par rig par bya'o//gang zhig nad chen pos btab ste/sman pa rnams kyis bye brag phye ste/bor ba de yang de ltar na nad de las yongasu thar bar 'gyur//de'i ngan 'gro'i skye ba thaMd 
38a3(rgyud): kyang rgyun 'chad par 'gyuro//shi 'phos nas 'jig rten gyi khams bde ba can du skye bar 'gyuro//de nyid de'i mngal gyis tha ma yin par rig par bya'o //de gang dang du skyes pa de dang der pad ma'i snying po kho na la rdzus te 
38a4(rgyud): skye bar 'gyuro//tshe rabs thams cad du yang tshe rabs dran par 'gyuro//sems can sdig pa byed pa gang dag shi ba'i dus byas pa/de dag gi don du nyungs dkar la lan cig yongasu brjod te/de dag gi rus gong rnaMs 
38a5(rgyud): kyi steng du dor na/de dag las thar bad 'gyuro//sems can dmyal ba dang dud 'gro'i skye gnas sam/gshin rje'i 'jig rten nam/yid tags sam de las gzhan pa ngan 'gro rnaMsu skye bar 
38a6(rgyud): gyur kyang rung ste/de gzungs 'di'i mthus ngan 'gro de dag las thar bar 'gyuro//de dag las thar nas kyang lhar skye bar bar 'gyur ro//shi 'phos nas kyang 'jig rten gyi khams bde pa can du skye bar 'gyuro//
38a7(rgyud): de nas kyang sangs rgyas kyi zhing tha dad pa sna tshogasu skye bar 'gyuro//der yang de bzhin gshegs pa rnams dang 'phrad par 'gyur/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis kyang dbugs 'byin par 'gyur//
38a8(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyis kyang lung ston par 'gyur/sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad du yang 'jig rten la snang ba byed par 'gyuro//phyag rgya bcing ba ni sor mo bcu'i thal mo sbyar ba'i sdig
38b1(rgyud): 'dzub gnyis bkug ste/the bong dang sbyar la gzungs 'di dran par bya'o//dkyil 'khor gru zhi yang byas te/me tog sna tshogs bkram pa la sna tshogs pa'i bdug pas bdug cing /pus mo g.yas pa'i lha ngas
38b2(rgyud): la gtsugs te/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad ting nge 'dzin gyis dmigs la/phyag rgya zhing de las gzungs brgyad rgya klag par bya'o//de ltar lan cig klags pa tsam gyis bye ba khrag khrig rgya stong phrag
38b3(rgyud): brgya bcu rtsa brgyad kyis gang ga'i klung gi bye ma snyed dang mnyam pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa la mchod cing sten bkur ba dang /ri mo byed do//de bzhin gshegs pa la mchog pa chen po'i sprin gyis mchod par 'gyuro//
38b4(rgyud): de kuni gyis kyang legaso zhes bya ba'i sgra brjod par 'gyuro//sems can de ni de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyis bdag nyid las skyes pa'i srasu rig par bya'o//sgrin pa med pa'i ye shes dang ldan
38b5(rgyud): zhing /byang chub chen po'i sems kyis rgyan dang mnyam par thob par 'gyuro//de la gzungs sgrub pa'i cho ga'i thabs 'di ni lha'i dbang po'i 'gro ba thams cad kyang sems can dmyal ba

94a7(rgyud): bdag nyid yongs skyed cing//lha'i rang bzhin sgrub pa yi//rin chen gzugs kyi phreng ba ni//chos rnams ma lus thob gyur te//ral gri mig sman ri lu dang//rkang pa byug dang gter chen dang//
94a8(rgyud): sa 'og grong dang bcud len dang//nam mkhar 'gro dang bcud len dang//de bzhin sa gzhan gro ba dang//zhi dang rgyas dang dbang don spyod//rmongs dang rangs dang skrad pa dang//deb dang dgug dang rigs sngags
94b1(rgyud): 'chang //srung dang phyir zlog chen po ste//zha dang rkugs dang sems rgyal dang //long dang sna tshogs nad skyed pa'i//sbyor ba rgya chen 'grub pa dang //mngon shes drug po yongs skye zhing //sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje yi//
94b2(rgyud): phyag rgya chen po thob gyur na//ltas mthong tsam la sogs pa yi//dngos grub 'phra mo smos ci dgos//sngags kyi sbyor ba skyed pa'i lha//dmigs pa'i mngon shes rtsa bar gyur pa yi//hU~M las skyes pa'i lha mo ni//
94b3(rgyud): sngon po zhal gsum phyag drug pa//g.yas pa ser zhing g.yon pa mar//sman gsum mig ni khro gnyer can//dmar ser gyen sra thod pas rgyan//rus pa'i sbas shing 'gying //thod pa'i phreng can klu'i sa brgyan//
94b4(rgyud): zhabs gnyis rkyang skum ra la rdzis//phyag gnyis g.yas kyi dang po na//rdo rje gnyis pa gri gug ste//g.yon gyi dang po sdig 'dzub ste//sbrul gyi zhags pa dkar po'o//yang na zhal kun snga sangs nyid//
94b5(rgyud): 'das ni 'ching dang dbang gi las//mtha' dag rdzogs par byed pa'o//sri ma las skyes pa'i lha mo ni/sngon mo zhal cig phyag gnyis sam//phyag g.yas rdo rje g.yon lcags sgrog//
94b6(rgyud): gcer bu rus pa'i rgyan ldan pa//gsad pa'i las rnams thams cad ni//rdo rje rnal 'byor ma 'dis sgrub//rdo rje bcom ldan rdo rje can//chos rnams thaMd 'byung byed cing //dngos grub myur du skyed pa yi//
94b7(rgyud): phyag rgya 'byung zhes bya ba ni//ting nge 'dzin la snyoms zhugs nas//yan lag phyag rgya bka' stsal pa//lag gnyis thal mo sbyar byas cing //slar la khu tshur bcang byas te//rgyab gnyis srad nas 'dzub mo
94b8(rgyud): sbyar byas cing //the chung 'dzub mo lcags sgrog bcings bya ste//kha'i thad dkar gzhag nas ni//rig pa'i phreng bas brjod pa yis//'dod pa thaMd 'grub par byed//phyag rgya bcings nas bsangs
95a1(rgyud): @#//nas ni//rjesu drang bar byas tsam gyis//kham du 'gro rde bar thar bar bya//gzhan gyi rigasngags gcod par byed//'phags ma rdo rje lu gu rgyud ma yi//rgyud kyi rigasngags las//lha dang phyag rgya
95a2(rgyud): rten la dbab pa yi//le'u ste gnyis pa'o////de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//rdzus kyi sbyor ba bla na med//mtshungs pa'i tshogs kun 'grub byed pa//rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma'i sa nyon//oM sarba ta 
95a3(rgyud): thA gA tA a b+hi shin tsa du ma//oM sarba ta thA g+ta tA a b+hi shi ka ta sa ma yA sri ye hUM//dbang skur ba ni bA na med//dri ma'i tshogs rnams dge byed cing//ji srid ye shes tshogs rnams rgyas nas su//rig pa'i
95a4(rgyud): rgyal po 'thob par byed//dza hUM baM ho//a badzra pus pe hUM swAhA/du pe  di pha/g+han d+he ni pi ti a hUM swAhA//oM A+oM kraM hrim sa la g+ha na bi ra ba re sha r+ya sa wa ri wa re/a a a a b+ha ti ri na ti u
95a5(rgyud): b+ha ta ra na te/a b+ha ta ra na tu sarba d+ha bdi ka lo ka pa la ki/la kaM pa ti/i mang b+ha li g+ti dan tu kha d+han du pi phan tu hUM phaT/kha khA/khA hI/kha hi/l+le l+le badzra da ka ad dza pa nya mi ta swAhA//ye shes sems
95a6(rgyud): dpa'i sbyor ba yis//tha dad rtog pa spong bar byed//mchod pa dang gtor ma yis//bar du gcog pa thams cad sel bar byed//oM ka le badzra saM laM b+ha wa ad mo ka ha~M//bag badzra saM b+ha wa ad mo ka ha~M/
95a7(rgyud): oM tsi ta badzra sarba tha ad mo ka ha~M//sku gsung gi thugs rdo rje yis//rnam par spras pa'i mtshan ma la//mtshan ma med pa rnam gnas shes dang mtshan ma rnam par dag//oM sarba ta tha ga ta dz+nya na
95a8(rgyud): badzra ad mo ka ha~M//gang zhig dbang pos g.yon tshon pa'i//mtshon cha mtshan nyid rnam shes dang//ye shes nyid kyi mngon gsum du//skye ba nyid the tsom med//de nas rgya chen sbyor ba'i gtso//
95b1(rgyud): 'khor lo'i cho ga bshad par bya//grog ris sam zhing shun la//gu gul g+hi wang tsan dan bcas//dbu skya ser pos 'khor lo ni//rtsibs bcu pad ma ni//mtha' nas med pa'i phreng bas skor//pad ma'i 
95b2(rgyud): 'dab ma bcu la ni//phyogs bcu kun du bcings pa ni//rdo rje la sogs bcu pa bri//de'i rgyab du phyogs bcu rnams//bcings pa'i sngags ni ram bzhin bri//'khor lo rtsibs rnams bcu pa ni//hUM gnyis 
95b3(rgyud): bar du sgrub bya'i ming //srung ba dang bcas bri bar bya//phyogs bzhir sngags dang phyag rgya dang //'khrur 'khor bcod dang //'bugs pa'i sngags su bcas pa bri//gzhan yang mtshams drug po ni//
95b4(rgyud): lha dang klu dang lha ma yin//gnod sbyin grul bum la sogs pa//'ching ba drug pa bri bar bya//lte bar sgrub bya'i gzugs rnyan ni//dkyil krung thugs kar thal mo sbyar//mdzes pa'i gzugs rnyan bri bar bya//
95b5(rgyud): rdo rjes gsang sngags phreng ba nyid//stong phrag gcig tu brjod byas nas//rdo rje'i stod pas stong byas te//gser sngags dro la nas lus sam dbul//gang bde gnas su gdags pa 
95b6(rgyud): bya//don du rnal 'byor srung don tu//ji ltar bsam pa kun grub ste//'jigs pa brgyad po med par byed//de nas gzhan yang rgyal po'i ras//gser pa tsar 
95b7(rgyud): dan ga bur dang //dri zhim rnams kyi byas nas ni//rgyu skar rgyal gyi nyi ma la//gur gu gis ni 'khor lo dri//dbus su pad ma 'dab brgyad pa'i//phyi rol mu khyud gsum ldan 
95b8(rgyud): pa'i//pad ma'i lte bar sgrub bya'i gzugs//lag na rdo rjes mtshon pa dri//de la snying pa'i sngags kyis bskor//de rjes lte ba'i mu khyud la//steng 'og bcing ba'i sngags

96a2(rgyud): rang nyid lha'i sbyor rdzogs bya//thugs kha'i sa bon 'og gis ni//nam mkhar 'khor lo sbyangs byas te//yi ge hUM gi bar du bsam//de yongs gyur la sku swogs sgom//de dbus shes rab rnam par sgom

96a4(rgyud): stong phrag zlas byas nas//gser swogs dril nas lus sam 'gum//gtags nas 'jigs byed skye ba//thams cad du ni srung bar byed//yung skar ba ni rnam lnga dang //dur ba'i snyam bcas pa la//

96a6(rgyud): gsang sngags stong phrog zlas byas te//shis pa'i gyur pa nyi ma la//mchod rten sbyin por gtags byas nas//mgo ba'i nad rnams chad par 'gyur//phrag dang khu tshur bcas pa la//gzugs
96a7(rgyud): sngags stong phrag gsum bzlas te//zla ba zas bzung dus su ni//nye bar snyen rdzogs byas nas su//rdzas ni shin tu sbas byas te//sna tshogs gnod pa gang dmigs pa'i//snying kha'i sngags ni snun par bya//
96a8(rgyud): gang dmigs ni rnam par 'jig/rab la gsar par gyur pa la//shes rab mtshan ldan khrus byas te//shis pa'i nyi ma la gal nas//bcing ba'i drangs pa'i 'dud byas te//stong phrag gcig
96b1(rgyud): gis sngags bzlas nas//lus sam 'gul lam spyi bor gtags//des ni li tshe lo stong phrag thub//yang na rang lus rnam grigs nas//chu'i tshogs kyis dag bcas te//nyi shu rtsa gcig drangs ldan pas//

96b3(rgyud): gos dang zas dang snod spyad dang //sman la sogs rdzas rnams dang //mi 'khrus rgyal po'i phyag rgya yis//srungs na cis kyang mi gnod do//seng ge rngam chen stabs kyis ni//'ug pa'i sgra gsum brjod 
96b4(rgyud): byas nas//phyi rol rag sngags stong phrag ni//du ma cis kyang mi tshugs so//klu'i bdag po gnas lus kyis//rtsa gcig rdo rje'i phyag rgya ni//de nas gsang sngags gnas brjod na//

96b6(rgyud): 'gyur//phyi rol la sogs kun spangs nas//snang ba yid kyis slags byas te//mi snang ba la mngon dmigs nas//srung ba rnams kyi dam pa bshad//de nas gzhan yang sbyor ba bshad//

96b8(rgyud): yang na rang gi khyim nyid du//srung ba maN+Dal legs par byug pa la//de nas lha'i sbyor bar sgrub//ras rims la sogs 'dun byas te//bde ba'i stan la dkyil krung gis//sdang nas

245a1(rgyud): //rtul//sngags dang skul tshig yang dag ldan//'dod na nor rnams myur du 'ong//dung kha rlung gi rkang pa la//steng du yam yas de steng ming//bskul tsig ldan na srod par 'gyuro
245a2(rgyud): me'i khang pa'i nang du ni//bsod byed pa'i dbang po la//de'i dbu na klu 'am byugs//de'i nang na sgrub tshig gis//mo tog 'dzom pa 'joms pas chu blang yi//rnam par klun pa'i lce la ni//sgrub
245a3(rgyud): bya'i ming ldan za byed kha//mi shes bslangs te gnan pa rkugs//'ug pheg bya rog sha ba khyi//byi la byi ba sbremong sbrul//rtsal ni mang du bsdus te yin//cher mas ye shes zlog pa ni//de ni myur
245a4(rgyud): du rengs yin//byil song khyim nas long spyod blang//rgya mtsho'i gling nas gser blangs te//rgyal pos bcus pa'i gzugs byas la//chu gang rtsang ma laswo mchod//skul tshig ldan pa'i snod du

245a6(rgyud): 'jug po la//'dod khams bdag po'i mtshan ma yis//rdzas la de 'dra'i gzugs bya ste//lung ba'i sa la de yul bya//nang du cug pa'i skul tshig gis//mngal gi nas 'phros par

245a8(rgyud): mun pa'i nang na rter na ma//de gzugs sa la de bra dri//byi ru sdong po zhon pa ste//sgrags dang ting 'dzin rab ldan skus//myur du gzhan gyi dran du 'gro//sgrib med 'pho ba'i skad ma//rus pa bsgrubs
245b1(rgyud): can thal ba la//rdo sdong bdal pa'i de mid bri//lung ba'i sa la de yul bya rlung gis mnan nas nor dang bral//gshog pa smug nag bdud kyi rta//rting lpags med pa'i snying po'o//de'i khar de cug ste/shel gyi
245b2(rgyud): 'od zer nub na 'chi//drod gsher nyid la khang pa 'dra//gzhon nu thal ba'i khang pa la//skul tshig sbyar nag gzhonu 'chi//de ni mo bsham 'dra ba yin//dri'u'i bu ni gzugs ldan pa//ri bong bu ni ra can

245b5(rgyud): bas chos kyi ston mo 'dzad//ci phyir de ni sgrag pa'i phyir//thabs kyi lam du 'dzud pa yin//thabs kyi cho 'phrul gang la yang //rten nas zhig nas thob par 'gyur//de yis lhag mthong rnyed par

245b8(rgyud): 'dul ba yin//gang dag phung po dang ni//dbyibs dang kha dog 'dul gis ni//mos pa dman pa 'dul ba yin//sku mdog phyag mtshan bdan khri dang //bzhugs tshul laswo stan pa dang //mu stegs can rnam

247b1(rgyud): 'thun par stod pa'i me tog char//sde dpon nag po chen po degs pa'i//sa bzhi mdzes par byas pa'i phyir stod pa//mi 'jigs seng ge nyon mongs tshogs las rgyal//sde dpon dpa'o bdud kyi

272b8(rgyud): gnyis pa'i gsum pa'i lnga pa ni//thug pa'i sgra ldan gnyis su bshad//gnyis po'i bzhi pa'i gsum pa ni//shin tu ring ba'i sgra dang ldan//rgyad pa'i mthar ldan sgra gnyis ni//shin tu dag 
273a1(rgyud): @#//po'i sgra dang ldan//gnyis pa lnga pa'i dang po gnyis//thung ba'i sgra dang ldan par bshad//gnyis pa'i gnyis pa dang po gnyis//cung zad drag po'i sgra dang ldan//'di ni 'khaur rwa bshad pa//
273a2(rgyud): de'i yi ger btu bar bya//gnyis pa bzhi pa'i gsum pa la//dang po gsum par mnan par bya//gnyis pa'i bzhi ni lnga par gzhag//gnyis pa'i lnga pa'i lnga pa la//dang po bcu gnyis yan lag 
273a3(rgyud): bkod//gnyis pa yi ni gsuM pa la//dang po bcu gcig bkod par bya//gnyis pa bzhi ba'i lnga pa gzhag//rgyad pa yi ni mthar ldan pa//dang po lnga pas yan lag rgyan//dang po thun zla ba yi mig//
273a4(rgyud): gnyis pa'i lnga bas thig ler gzhag//cung zad ring ba'i sgra dang ldan//gnyis pa'i lnga pa'i gnyis pa la//gnyis pa'i gsum pa'i dang po gzhag//shin tu drag pa'i sgra dang ldan//rgyad pa'i 'tha'
273a5(rgyud): can gsum pa la//gnyis pa'i lnga pa'i gsuM pa gdags//sde tshan rgyad pa'i mthar ldan pa//cung zad ring ba'i sgra ldan//zhes bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal to//dpal nag po
273a6(rgyud): chen po'i rgyud dur khrod nag po zhes bya ba/las yi ge btu ba'i le'u zhes bya ste dgu pa'o////de nas dpal nag po chen pos gsol pa//bcom ldan 'das sku gsung thugs kyi rang bzhin las//
273a7(rgyud): dpal ldan nag po chen po yi//dngos grub nges par 'grub pa dang //cho ga'i las rnams rdzogs pa yi//'grub pa'i rgyu ni ci ltar lags//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka'i stsal pa//sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje dang
273a8(rgyud): dpaldan nag po chen po yi/dngos grub nye bar 'grub pa'i rgyu//dam tshig cho ga 'di dag la//gnas par byed pa'i rnal 'byor bas//nag po'i bsam rtan thob par 'gyur//zhes bcom ldan 'dasakyis bka' stsal te
273b1(rgyud): de nas dpal nag po chen pos gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das dam tshig cho ga ji ltar lags//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/bla ma sangs rgyas thaMd dang//dpal ldan nag po chen po yi//rtag tu yid 
273b2(rgyud): la gnas par bya//dpal ldan nag po chenapo yi//sngags dang phyag rgya bsam rtan la//yid kyis som nyi med par bya//yongs su ma smin seMn dang//bla ma rdo rje 'dzin spong ba//dam tshig bral bar byed pa dang//
273b3(rgyud): rgyal ba'i bstan pa 'jig byed pa//dpal ldan po chen po yi//bsam rtan sngags ldan ldan gyur nas//snying rjes bral bar ma byas na//rnal 'byor nges par bshad par bya//dpal ldan nagapo sgrub pa 
273b4(rgyud): des//sku dang gri glag thod pa dang//'thun pa'i dam rdzas la sogs pa//de dag rtag tu sba bar bya//brgyud dang sngags kyi don rnams dang//nag po'i bsam rtan cho gar byas//kun la
273b5(rgyud): bstan par mi bya ste//bdud rtsi lnga dang sreg rdzas dang//sha chen nya sha chang dang ni//dug gi la du la sogs pa//'thun par dam rdzas thams cad ni//rtag tu rnal 'byor pa yis sten//dpal ldan nag
273b6(rgyud): po chen po yi//sku dang gri gug thod pa dang//ne rid su la sogs pa yi//bla mas rab tu gnas byas te//dus gsum 'thun pa'i rdzas kyis mchod//rtag tu tshogs kyi 'khor lo yis//dpal ldan nag po
273b7(rgyud): sgrub 'dod pas//bla mas rjes su gnang bar byed//des ni dpal ldan bo sgrub//'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//bstan pa gnas par byed pa ni//dam tshig chen po rab tu bshad//kun la stan 
273b8(rgyud): par mi bya ste//gal te rmongs par stan gyur na//e rdza ka'i 'bras bu ltar//mgo bo lus po 'gas 'gyur te//dmyal bar nges par ltung bar 'gyur//zhes bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//
274a1(rgyud): /dang ldan nag po chen po'i rgyud//dur khrod chen po zhes bya ba las//dam tshig gi le'u ste bcu pa'o////dpaldan nag po chen po'i rgyud //drag po rtag pa dur khrod chen po zhes bya brdzogs s+h+yo////
274a2(rgyud): rgya gar skad du/shri mahA ka la d+ha ra/bod skad du/'phags pa dpal mgon po nag po zhes bya ba'i gzungs//sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thaMd la phyag 'tshalo //'di skad bdag gis thos pa'i dus cig na bcom ldan 'das rdo rje'i
274a3(rgyud): bdan la bdud btul te/mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas nas/ring po ma lon par zhig nas//byang chub kyi shing drung du bzhugs nas/bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der logasu lha'i sde dpon chen po tshogs kyi bdag po ma ha ka la
274a4(rgyud): 'dod pa khams kyi dbang dang phyug dbang sgyur ma dpal mgon nag po chen pos //bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal te //phyogs cig tu 'dug nas 'di skad ces gsol to//btsun pa bcom ldan
274a5(rgyud): 'das bdag gi rig pa 'di 'bul gyis/bzhes su gsol/oM shri ma ha ka la g+ha na g+hu ti swahA//oM shri mam mahA ka la g+hane ye/na ma yo na ma yo na kron Ni na/tad+ya thA/oM hi le ha le/gi de gi ti
274a6(rgyud): ne/cid te cid te ni swahA//'di bton pas gtsug lag khang dang/dkyil 'khor gyi srung ma /dug gsum gyi sman pa /rigs 'dzin yongs su srung shing //rnal 'byor kun gyi dra bla//bdud 'dul
274a7(rgyud): gyi dbang phyug/srin po lang ka pu ri'i grong khyer rtan nas brlag pa//lha ma yin gyi bu mo nam mkha'i klog ma srin bdul du brlag pa /dkon mchog gsum gyi bstan pa srung bar
274a8(rgyud): khas blangs shing //dam bcas pa//'phags pa mgon po chen po zhes bya ba'i gzungs rdzogs s+h+yo////bkra shis par gyur cig//
